     1→# Memory
     2→
     3→## User Preferences
     4→
     5→- **Commit approval**: Always present the full commit commands to the user for physical review BEFORE executing them. Do not run jj commit / git commit until the user explicitly approves.
     6→- **Versioning**: Every plan must start with a version bump per notes/README.md "Versioning during development". Get user approval on single-step vs multi-step approach before starting.
     7→- **Pre-commit checklist**: Before committing, run: tests, clippy, fmt, install the app, then retest.
     8→
     9→## Feedback
    10→
    11→- [Always include ochid trailer in commits](feedback_ochid_trailer.md)
    12→
    13→## Lessons Learned
    14→
    15→- **Avoid duplicate commits in .claude repo**: Committing the bot session repo multiple times in one session can create duplicate-titled commits with other session commits interleaved, making squashing non-trivial. Prefer a single commit to .claude at session end.
    16→- **ochid trailers need updating after squash**: When squashing changes the target commit in .claude, the ochid reference in the app repo must be updated to point at the correct changeID.
    17→- **Don't modify .claude repo programmatically mid-session**: Session data updates continuously, so bot-initiated changes to .claude can create circular modifications.
    18→
    19→## Key Files
    20→
    21→- notes/README.md — contains versioning workflow, todo format rules
    22→- notes/todo.md — near-term task tracking
    23→- notes/chores-01.md — chore details and reference links
    24→- CLAUDE.md — dual repo commit/push workflow
    25→
```

     1→---
     2→name: Always include ochid trailer in commits
     3→description: App repo commits must include ochid trailer referencing the .claude repo counterpart changeID
     4→type: feedback
     5→---
     6→
     7→Always include the `ochid` trailer when committing to the app repo. The trailer links the commit to its .claude session counterpart.
     8→
     9→**Why:** The ochid trailer is part of the inter-repo cross-reference convention. Missing it breaks the ability to correlate app and session commits.
    10→
    11→**How to apply:** Before proposing any app repo commit, check `jj log -R .claude --limit 1` to get the current .claude working copy changeID, then add `ochid: /.claude/<changeID>` as a trailer in the commit body.
    12→
```

```
     1→# Notes
     2→
     3→This directory contains various notes and documentation related to the project.
     4→Each file is organized by topic for easy reference.
     5→
     6→By default there are chores-*.md and todo.md. Chores are general notes
     7→about tasks and todo.md contains short term tasks and their status.
     8→
     9→In the future we I expect we may want to create a "notes"
    10→database to better manage the information, TBD.
    11→
    12→Examples chore file:
    13→```
    14→# Chores-01.md
    15→ 
    16→General maintenance tasks and considerations for the project see other files for
    17→more specific topics. A chore in a chores file provides quick information on the
    18→how and why of a particular chore.
    19→
    20→## Create a binary that lists jj info 
    21→
    22→This binary should list the changeID, commitID, and description title
    23→and using `jj-lib`
    24→```
    25→
    26→## Versioning during development
    27→
    28→This is using jujustiu, jj + git and we'll see how it goes. Below is my
    29→git workflow, jj will be different but we'll have to discover that as
    30→we go.
    31→
    32→Every plan must start with a version bump. Choose the approach based on scope:
    33→
    34→- **Single-step** (recommended for mechanical/focused changes): bump directly to
    35→  `X.Y.Z`, implement in one commit. Simpler history.
    36→- **Multi-step** (for exploratory/large changes): bump to `X.Y.Z-devN`, implement
    37→  across multiple commits, final commit removes `-devN`.
    38→
    39→The plan should recommend one approach and get user approval before starting.
    40→
    41→For multi-step:
    42→1. Bump version to `X.Y.Z-devN` with a plan and commit as a chore marker
    43→2. Implement in one or more `-devN` commits (bump N as needed)
    44→3. Final commit removes `-devN`, updates todo/chores — this is the "done" marker
    45→
    46→The final release commit (without `-devN`) signals completion rather than amending
    47→prior commits. This keeps the git history readable and makes it easy to see which
    48→commits were exploratory vs final.
    49→
    50→## Todo format
    51→
    52→Todo.md contains two main sections "Todo" and "Done" each item is a
    53→short explanations of a tasks and links to more details using 1 or more
    54→references.
    55→
    56→Multiple references must be separated: `[2],[3]` not `[2,3]` or `[2][3]`.
    57→In markdown, `[2,3]` is a single ref key (won't resolve) and `[2][3]`
    58→is parsed as display text `2` with ref key `3` (so `[2]` won't resolve).
    59→
    60→Examples:
    61→
    62→# Todo
    63→- Add new feature X [details](features.md#feature-x)
    64→- Fix bug Y [1]
    65→
    66→# Done
    67→- Fixed issue Z [2],[3]
    68→
    69→[1]: bugs.md#bug-y
    70→[2]: issues.md#issue-z
    71→[3]: fixes.md#fix-z
    72→
```

Here's where things stand:

**Project**: `vc-x1` — a Rust CLI tool (v0.6.0) for managing jj+git repo workflows. Single source file at `src/main.rs`, using `jj-lib`, `pollster`, and `libc`.

**Recent work**: The `finalize` subcommand was completed and released as v0.6.0. It handles squash/push of the `.claude` session repo with daemonization, `--ignore-immutable`, unique log paths, and delay support.

**Current state**: Clean working copy on top of `main`. No in-progress tasks.

**Open todos**:
- Add integration tests for `finalize` (using tempfile crate)
- Investigate jj commit organization (visibility gap vs `gitk --all`)

**Key conventions I'll follow**:
- Dual repo workflow (app at `.`, session at `.claude`)
- Version bump before any changes
- Pre-commit: fmt, clippy, test, install, retest
- Present commit commands for approval before running
- Include `ochid` trailer in app repo commits
- Single `.claude` commit at session end

Ready to go — what would you like to work on?

```
     1→use std::io::Write;
     2→use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
     3→use std::process::{ExitCode, Stdio};
     4→
     5→use jj_lib::config::StackedConfig;
     6→use jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex;
     7→use jj_lib::object_id::ObjectId;
     8→use jj_lib::repo::{Repo, StoreFactories};
     9→use jj_lib::revset::{RevsetExpression, SymbolResolver};
    10→use jj_lib::settings::UserSettings;
    11→use jj_lib::workspace::{Workspace, default_working_copy_factories};
    12→use pollster::FutureExt;
    13→
    14→const USAGE: &str = "\
    15→Usage: vc-x1 [OPTIONS] <COMMAND>
    16→
    17→Commands:
    18→  list [<path>]       List commits in a jj repo (defaults to current directory)
    19→  finalize [OPTIONS]  Squash working copy into target commit (daemonizes by default)
    20→
    21→Options:
    22→  -V, --version  Print version
    23→  -h, --help     Print this help message";
    24→
    25→const FINALIZE_USAGE: &str = "\
    26→Usage: vc-x1 finalize [OPTIONS]
    27→
    28→Squash source revision into target revision, optionally push afterward.
    29→By default, daemonizes and returns immediately.
    30→
    31→Options:
    32→  --repo <path>      Path to jj repo (default: current directory)
    33→  --source <revset>  Source revision to squash (default: @)
    34→  --target <revset>  Target revision to squash into (default: @-)
    35→  --delay <seconds>  Seconds to wait before squashing (default: 1)
    36→  --push             Push after squashing
    37→  --log <path>       Log file path (default: /tmp/vc-x1-finalize-<timestamp-millis>.log)
    38→  --foreground       Run in foreground instead of daemonizing
    39→  -h, --help         Print this help message";
    40→
    41→#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
    42→enum Command {
    43→    Version,
    44→    Help,
    45→    FinalizeHelp,
    46→    List { path: PathBuf },
    47→    Finalize(FinalizeOpts),
    48→}
    49→
    50→#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
    51→struct FinalizeOpts {
    52→    repo: PathBuf,
    53→    source: String,
    54→    target: String,
    55→    delay_secs: f64,
    56→    push: bool,
    57→    log: PathBuf,
    58→    foreground: bool,
    59→    exec: bool,
    60→}
    61→
    62→impl Default for FinalizeOpts {
    63→    fn default() -> Self {
    64→        Self {
    65→            repo: PathBuf::from("."),
    66→            source: "@".to_string(),
    67→            target: "@-".to_string(),
    68→            delay_secs: 1.0,
    69→            push: false,
    70→            log: PathBuf::from(format!(
    71→                "/tmp/vc-x1-finalize-{}.log",
    72→                std::time::SystemTime::now()
    73→                    .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
    74→                    .unwrap_or_default()
    75→                    .as_millis()
    76→            )),
    77→            foreground: false,
    78→            exec: false,
    79→        }
    80→    }
    81→}
    82→
    83→fn parse_finalize_args<I>(args: &mut I) -> Result<Command, String>
    84→where
    85→    I: Iterator<Item = String>,
    86→{
    87→    let mut opts = FinalizeOpts::default();
    88→
    89→    while let Some(arg) = args.next() {
    90→        match arg.as_str() {
    91→            "--help" | "-h" => return Ok(Command::FinalizeHelp),
    92→            "--repo" => {
    93→                opts.repo = PathBuf::from(
    94→                    args.next()
    95→                        .ok_or_else(|| "--repo requires a value".to_string())?,
    96→                );
    97→            }
    98→            "--source" => {
    99→                opts.source = args
   100→                    .next()
   101→                    .ok_or_else(|| "--source requires a value".to_string())?;
   102→            }
   103→            "--target" => {
   104→                opts.target = args
   105→                    .next()
   106→                    .ok_or_else(|| "--target requires a value".to_string())?;
   107→            }
   108→            "--delay" => {
   109→                let val = args
   110→                    .next()
   111→                    .ok_or_else(|| "--delay requires a value".to_string())?;
   112→                opts.delay_secs = val
   113→                    .parse()
   114→                    .map_err(|_| format!("invalid delay value: {val}"))?;
   115→            }
   116→            "--push" => opts.push = true,
   117→            "--log" => {
   118→                opts.log = PathBuf::from(
   119→                    args.next()
   120→                        .ok_or_else(|| "--log requires a value".to_string())?,
   121→                );
   122→            }
   123→            "--foreground" => opts.foreground = true,
   124→            "--exec" => opts.exec = true,
   125→            other => {
   126→                return Err(format!(
   127→                    "unknown finalize option: {other}\n\n{FINALIZE_USAGE}"
   128→                ));
   129→            }
   130→        }
   131→    }
   132→
   133→    Ok(Command::Finalize(opts))
   134→}
   135→
   136→fn parse_args<I>(args: I) -> Result<Command, String>
   137→where
   138→    I: IntoIterator<Item = String>,
   139→{
   140→    let mut args = args.into_iter();
   141→    let _program = args.next();
   142→
   143→    match args.next().as_deref() {
   144→        Some("--version" | "-V") => Ok(Command::Version),
   145→        Some("--help" | "-h" | "help") => Ok(Command::Help),
   146→        Some("list") => {
   147→            let path = match args.next() {
   148→                Some(p) => PathBuf::from(p),
   149→                None => std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
   150→            };
   151→            Ok(Command::List { path })
   152→        }
   153→        Some("finalize") => parse_finalize_args(&mut args),
   154→        Some(other) => Err(format!("unknown command: {other}\n\n{USAGE}")),
   155→        None => Ok(Command::Help),
   156→    }
   157→}
   158→
   159→fn list(path: &Path) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   160→    let config = StackedConfig::with_defaults();
   161→    let settings = UserSettings::from_config(config)?;
   162→    let store_factories = StoreFactories::default();
   163→    let working_copy_factories = default_working_copy_factories();
   164→
   165→    let workspace = Workspace::load(&settings, path, &store_factories, &working_copy_factories)?;
   166→    let repo = workspace.repo_loader().load_at_head().block_on()?;
   167→
   168→    let expression = RevsetExpression::all();
   169→    let no_extensions: &[Box<dyn jj_lib::revset::SymbolResolverExtension>] = &[];
   170→    let symbol_resolver = SymbolResolver::new(repo.as_ref(), no_extensions);
   171→    let resolved = expression.resolve_user_expression(repo.as_ref(), &symbol_resolver)?;
   172→    let revset = resolved.evaluate(repo.as_ref())?;
   173→
   174→    let root_commit_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();
   175→
   176→    for result in revset.iter() {
   177→        let commit_id = result?;
   178→        if commit_id == root_commit_id {
   179→            continue;
   180→        }
   181→        let commit = repo.store().get_commit(&commit_id)?;
   182→
   183→        let change_hex = encode_reverse_hex(commit.change_id().as_bytes());
   184→        let change_short = &change_hex[..change_hex.len().min(12)];
   185→
   186→        let commit_hex = commit.id().hex();
   187→        let commit_short = &commit_hex[..commit_hex.len().min(12)];
   188→
   189→        let first_line = commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or("");
   190→
   191→        println!("{} {} {}", change_short, commit_short, first_line);
   192→    }
   193→
   194→    Ok(())
   195→}
   196→
   197→fn log_msg(log: &Path, msg: &str) {
   198→    let nanos = std::time::SystemTime::now()
   199→        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
   200→        .unwrap_or_default()
   201→        .as_nanos();
   202→    let pid = std::process::id();
   203→    let line = format!("[{nanos}] pid={pid} {msg}\n");
   204→    if let Ok(mut f) = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
   205→        .create(true)
   206→        .append(true)
   207→        .open(log)
   208→    {
   209→        let _ = f.write_all(line.as_bytes());
   210→    }
   211→}
   212→
   213→fn run_jj(args: &[&str], log: &Path) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   214→    let args_str = args.join(" ");
   215→    log_msg(log, &format!("run_jj: jj {args_str}"));
   216→    let output = std::process::Command::new("jj")
   217→        .args(args)
   218→        .output()
   219→        .map_err(|e| format!("failed to run jj: {e}"))?;
   220→    let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
   221→    let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
   222→    if !stdout.is_empty() {
   223→        log_msg(log, &format!("run_jj: stdout: {stdout}"));
   224→    }
   225→    if !stderr.is_empty() {
   226→        log_msg(log, &format!("run_jj: stderr: {stderr}"));
   227→    }
   228→    if !output.status.success() {
   229→        return Err(format!("jj {args_str} failed (exit {}): {stderr}", output.status).into());
   230→    }
   231→    Ok(())
   232→}
   233→
   234→fn finalize_exec(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   235→    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("finalize_exec: starting opts={opts:?}"));
   236→
   237→    // Sleep to let trailing writes settle
   238→    let delay = std::time::Duration::from_secs_f64(opts.delay_secs);
   239→    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("finalize_exec: sleeping {delay:?}"));
   240→    std::thread::sleep(delay);
   241→
   242→    // Squash source into target
   243→    let repo_str = opts.repo.to_string_lossy();
   244→    run_jj(
   245→        &[
   246→            "squash",
   247→            "--ignore-immutable",
   248→            "--use-destination-message",
   249→            "--from",
   250→            &opts.source,
   251→            "--into",
   252→            &opts.target,
   253→            "-R",
   254→            &repo_str,
   255→        ],
   256→        &opts.log,
   257→    )?;
   258→
   259→    if opts.push {
   260→        // Advance main bookmark to target, then push
   261→        run_jj(
   262→            &[
   263→                "bookmark",
   264→                "set",
   265→                "main",
   266→                "-r",
   267→                &opts.target,
   268→                "-R",
   269→                &repo_str,
   270→            ],
   271→            &opts.log,
   272→        )?;
   273→        run_jj(&["git", "push", "-R", &repo_str], &opts.log)?;
   274→    }
   275→
   276→    log_msg(&opts.log, "finalize_exec: done");
   277→    Ok(())
   278→}
   279→
   280→fn build_exec_args(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Vec<String> {
   281→    let mut args = vec![
   282→        "finalize".to_string(),
   283→        "--exec".to_string(),
   284→        "--repo".to_string(),
   285→        opts.repo.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   286→        "--source".to_string(),
   287→        opts.source.clone(),
   288→        "--target".to_string(),
   289→        opts.target.clone(),
   290→        "--delay".to_string(),
   291→        opts.delay_secs.to_string(),
   292→        "--log".to_string(),
   293→        opts.log.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   294→    ];
   295→    if opts.push {
   296→        args.push("--push".to_string());
   297→    }
   298→    args
   299→}
   300→
   301→/// Spawn a detached child process with `--exec` and return immediately.
   302→/// The child re-enters `main()`, parses `--exec`, and `finalize()` routes
   303→/// it to `finalize_exec()` where the actual work happens.
   304→fn daemonize(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   305→    log_msg(
   306→        &opts.log,
   307→        &format!("daemonize: parent starting opts={opts:?}"),
   308→    );
   309→
   310→    let exe = std::env::current_exe()?;
   311→    let args = build_exec_args(opts);
   312→    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("daemonize: exe={exe:?} args={args:?}"));
   313→
   314→    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new(exe);
   315→    cmd.args(&args).stdin(Stdio::null());
   316→
   317→    #[cfg(unix)]
   318→    {
   319→        use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;
   320→        unsafe {
   321→            cmd.pre_exec(|| {
   322→                libc::setsid();
   323→                Ok(())
   324→            });
   325→        }
   326→    }
   327→
   328→    let child = cmd.spawn()?;
   329→    log_msg(
   330→        &opts.log,
   331→        &format!("daemonize: spawned child pid={}", child.id()),
   332→    );
   333→    eprintln!(
   334→        "finalize: daemonized (pid {}), log: {}",
   335→        child.id(),
   336→        opts.log.display()
   337→    );
   338→    Ok(())
   339→}
   340→
   341→fn finalize(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   342→    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("finalize: entry opts={opts:?}"));
   343→    let result = if opts.foreground || opts.exec {
   344→        finalize_exec(opts)
   345→    } else {
   346→        daemonize(opts)
   347→    };
   348→    match &result {
   349→        Ok(()) => log_msg(&opts.log, "finalize: exit ok"),
   350→        Err(e) => log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("finalize: exit err={e}")),
   351→    }
   352→    result
   353→}
   354→
   355→fn main() -> ExitCode {
   356→    let cmd = match parse_args(std::env::args()) {
   357→        Ok(cmd) => cmd,
   358→        Err(e) => {
   359→            eprintln!("error: {e}");
   360→            return ExitCode::FAILURE;
   361→        }
   362→    };
   363→
   364→    match cmd {
   365→        Command::Version => {
   366→            println!("vc-x1 {}", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"));
   367→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
   368→        }
   369→        Command::Help => {
   370→            println!("{USAGE}");
   371→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
   372→        }
   373→        Command::FinalizeHelp => {
   374→            println!("{FINALIZE_USAGE}");
   375→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
   376→        }
   377→        Command::List { path } => {
   378→            if let Err(e) = list(&path) {
   379→                eprintln!("error: {e}");
   380→                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
   381→            }
   382→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
   383→        }
   384→        Command::Finalize(opts) => {
   385→            log_msg(&opts.log, "main: finalize entry");
   386→            match finalize(&opts) {
   387→                Ok(()) => {
   388→                    log_msg(&opts.log, "main: finalize exit ok");
   389→                    ExitCode::SUCCESS
   390→                }
   391→                Err(e) => {
   392→                    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("main: finalize exit err={e}"));
   393→                    eprintln!("error: {e}");
   394→                    ExitCode::FAILURE
   395→                }
   396→            }
   397→        }
   398→    }
   399→}
   400→

   404→

   548→    #[test]
   549→    fn build_exec_args_roundtrip() {
   550→        let opts = FinalizeOpts {
   551→            repo: PathBuf::from(".claude"),
   552→            source: "@".to_string(),
   553→            target: "@-".to_string(),
   554→            delay_secs: 2.0,
   555→            push: true,
   556→            log: PathBuf::from("/tmp/test.log"),
   557→            foreground: false,
   558→            exec: false,
   559→        };
   560→        let exec_args = build_exec_args(&opts);
   561→        // Parse them back (prepend program name)
   562→        let mut full_args = vec!["vc-x1".to_string()];
   563→        full_args.extend(exec_args);
   564→        let result = parse_args(full_args).unwrap();
   565→        if let Command::Finalize(parsed) = result {
   566→            assert_eq!(parsed.repo, opts.repo);
   567→            assert_eq!(parsed.source, opts.source);
   568→            assert_eq!(parsed.target, opts.target);
   569→            assert_eq!(parsed.delay_secs, opts.delay_secs);
   570→            assert_eq!(parsed.push, opts.push);
   571→            assert_eq!(parsed.log, opts.log);
   572→            assert!(parsed.exec);
   573→        } else {
   574→            panic!("expected Finalize");
   575→        }
   576→    }
   577→
   578→    #[test]
   579→    fn parse_finalize_unknown_opt() {

   581→        assert!(result.is_err());
   582→        assert!(result.unwrap_err().contains("unknown finalize option"));
   583→    }
   584→}

   115→
   116→## Use commit titles to cross-reference changeIDs
   117→
   118→This was tried but I decided footers were more flexible and less noice than the commit title.
   119→See [ChangeID footer syntax](#changeid-footer-syntax) for the final approach.
   120→
   121→A **critical item** in this experiment is each commit can contain the changeID
   122→of the other commit. Here I'm trying out providing both "this commit id" (tcid)
   123→and "other commit id" (ocid) in the commit title to make it easier for tools to
   124→navigate and synchronize the two repos.
   125→
   126→title syntax: `<tcid>-<ocid>-<short description>`
   127→
   128→ - `<tcid>` is the jj change ID of this commit and is never `none`.
   129→ - `<ocid>` is the jj change ID of the other commit and may be `none` if the bot was not involved.
   130→ - `<short description>` is a brief one line description of the chore.
   131→
   132→The intent is that these might be able to make the tool that shows the
   133→evolution of the code more clear by showing the relationship between the two repos
   134→
   135→## zznknssm-none-Validate changeID consistency
   136→
   137→Validate that when we clone a vc repo that the changeID remains the same
   138→between the original and the clone for both the main and the claude repos.
   139→

   195→$ jj st

   198→Parent commit (@-): ponzrznv 90244032 main main@origin | Initial Commit
   199→wink@3900x 26-03-04T18:58:50.893Z:~/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/.claude (main)
   200→```

   203→`ponzrznv` for .claude:

   205→wink@3900x 26-03-04T18:58:50.893Z:~/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/.claude (main)
   206→$ cd ../../vc-x1.ok/

   211→Parent commit (@-): qvnyrzox 5bb219d1 main | Initial Commit

   215→$ jj st

   217→Working copy  (@) : kttvktmm 0c376c25 (empty) (no description set)
   218→Parent commit (@-): ponzrznv 90244032 main | Initial Commit
   219→wink@3900x 26-03-04T19:00:56.656Z:~/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1.ok/.claude ((main))
   220→```
   221→
   222→## Use footers to track changeIDs or notes
   223→
   224→> **Note:** The original approach used markdown reference-link syntax
   225→> in commit footers. We now use
   226→> [git trailers](https://git-scm.com/docs/git-interpret-trailers)
   227→> (`key: value` format) instead, as they are a standard convention
   228→> and parseable by `git interpret-trailers` and other tools.
   229→
   230→Since jj changeIDs are generated at `jj git init` time (not stored in the
   231→repo), changeID references are only resolvable by tools that have access
   232→to the local jj repo (e.g. vc-x1).
   233→
   234→## Git trailer convention
   235→
   236→We use [git trailers](https://git-scm.com/docs/git-interpret-trailers)
   237→in commit messages for inter-repo cross-references. Trailers appear as
   238→blank-line-separated `key: value` lines at the end of the commit body.
   239→
   240→### ochid (Other Change ID)
   241→
   242→The `ochid` trailer links a commit to its counterpart in another repo
   243→within the workspace. The value is a workspace-root-relative path
   244→followed by the jj changeID:
   245→
   246→- `ochid: /changeID` — references the workspace-root repo (vc-x1)
   247→- `ochid: /.claude/changeID` — references the .claude sub-repo
   248→
   249→Example commit message:
   250→```
   251→Add jj tips for git users to README
   252→
   253→Add jj tips section to README explaining why jj log
   254→shows fewer commits than gitk.
   255→
   256→ochid: /.claude/xvzvruqo
   257→```
   258→
   259→### ChangeID path syntax
   260→
   261→All changeID paths are **workspace-root relative** (start with `/`):
   262→
   263→- `/` is the workspace root (the app repo, vc-x1)
   264→- `/.claude` is the bot session sub-repo
   265→
   266→This means `ochid: /wtpmottv` refers to a change in the app repo,
   267→**not** the .claude repo. The leading `/` anchors to the workspace
   268→root, not the current repo.
   269→
   270→### .vc-config.toml
   271→
   272→Each repo contains a `.vc-config.toml` that identifies its location
   273→within the workspace. This avoids repeating the workspace-path in
   274→every commit trailer.
   275→
   276→```toml
   277→# In vc-x1 (workspace root):
   278→[workspace]
   279→path = "/"
   280→
   281→# In .claude (sub-repo):
   282→[workspace]
   283→path = "/.claude"
   284→```
   285→
   286→Tools can read `.vc-config.toml` to resolve changeID paths in
   287→`ochid` trailers to the correct local repo.
   288→
   289→## Create a binary that lists jj info
   290→
   291→This binary should list the changeID, commitID, and description title
   292→and using `jj-lib`
   293→
   294→### Implementation
   295→
   296→Created a Rust binary (`src/main.rs`) using `jj-lib` 0.39.0 that:
   297→
   298→1. Opens the jj workspace from the current directory (or a path argument)
   299→2. Loads the repo at head via `Workspace::load()` and `RepoLoader::load_at_head()`
   300→3. Evaluates `RevsetExpression::all()` to iterate all commits
   301→4. For each commit (excluding the root), prints: changeID (reverse hex, 12 chars),
   302→   commitID (hex, 12 chars), and first line of description
   303→
   304→Key dependencies: `jj-lib = "0.39.0"`, `pollster = "0.4"` (for async `.block_on()`).
   305→
   306→Usage:
   307→```
   308→cargo run            # uses current directory
   309→cargo run -- /path   # uses specified path
   310→```
   311→
   312→## Finalize subcommand for session repo coherence
   313→
   314→### Problem
   315→
   316→When the bot commits and pushes the `.claude` session repo as its last action,
   317→the act of committing itself generates more `.jsonl` session data. This means
   318→the commit is always missing the trailing writes — information from commit N
   319→shows up in commit N+1.
   320→
   321→### Discovery
   322→
   323→We tested a `nohup bash -c "sleep N; jj squash; ..." &` pattern to delay the
   324→squash until after trailing writes settle. Findings:
   325→
   326→- **0.5s** is the minimum reliable delay; 0.25s also worked, 0.1s did not
   327→- **Shell `&` operator** triggers Claude Code's safety prompt ("shell operators
   328→  that require approval") — cannot be auto-approved via permission rules
   329→- **`run_in_background`** (Claude Code's built-in) doesn't help because it
   330→  blocks the bash slot and waits, so the session finishes writing before the
   331→  sleep even starts — no trailing writes to capture
   332→
   333→### Solution: `vc-x1 finalize` subcommand
   334→
   335→A Rust binary can daemonize itself via `std::process::Command` (detached spawn)
   336→without needing shell `&`. The permission rule `Bash(vc-x1 finalize *)` works
   337→cleanly.
   338→
   339→**Usage:**
   340→```
   341→vc-x1 finalize --repo .claude --delay 1 --push
   342→```
   343→
   344→**Options:**
   345→- `--repo <path>` — path to jj repo (default: `.`)
   346→- `--source <revset>` — revision to squash (default: `@`)
   347→- `--target <revset>` — revision to squash into (default: `@-`)
   348→- `--delay <seconds>` — seconds to wait before squashing (default: `1`)
   349→- `--push` — push after squashing
   350→- `--log <path>` — log file (default: `/tmp/vc-x1-finalize-<timestamp-millis>.log`)
   351→- `--foreground` — skip daemonization, run in foreground
   352→
   353→### Implementation plan
   354→
   355→- **0.6.0-dev1** (done): arg parsing for finalize subcommand
   356→- **0.6.0-dev2** (done): daemonize — binary re-spawns itself detached with
   357→  internal `--exec` flag, parent returns immediately. Added `log_msg` helper
   358→  for nanosecond-timestamped append-mode logging throughout the flow.
   359→- **0.6.0-dev3** (done): implement finalize logic — daemonized child sleeps,
   360→  then shells out to `jj squash`, `jj bookmark set`, `jj git push`
   361→- **0.6.0-dev4** (done): add `--ignore-immutable` to squash, unique log paths
   362→  per invocation using timestamp-millis
   363→- **0.6.0**: finalize release — remove `-devN`, update docs/todo/chores
   364→
   365→### BREAKING-CHANGE trailer
   366→
   367→During this work we confirmed that `BREAKING CHANGE:` (with space) is the only
   368→space-separated git trailer key allowed per the Conventional Commits spec. We
   369→adopted the hyphenated form `BREAKING-CHANGE:` as it's also valid and avoids
   370→the space ambiguity.
   371→
```

Here's my proposed plan. This is multi-step (refactoring + new feature), so I'd recommend the **multi-step** approach with `0.7.0-devN`.

## Plan: `vc-x1 desc` — display commit description by changeID

### Steps

| Step | Version | Description |
|------|---------|-------------|
| 1 | 0.7.0-dev1 | Refactor `list` into `src/list.rs` — extract `list()` fn and its tests |
| 2 | 0.7.0-dev2 | Refactor `finalize` into `src/finalize.rs` — extract `FinalizeOpts`, parsing, `finalize()`, `finalize_exec()`, `daemonize()`, `build_exec_args()`, `run_jj()`, `log_msg()`, and tests |
| 3 | 0.7.0-dev3 | Implement `desc` in `src/desc.rs` — resolve a changeID to a commit via jj-lib and print the full description |
| 4 | 0.7.0 | Final release — remove `-devN`, update todo/chores |

### `desc` subcommand design

```
Usage: vc-x1 desc <chid> [OPTIONS]

Display the full description of a commit identified by its changeID.

Arguments:
  <chid>            The jj changeID (or prefix) to look up

Options:
  --repo <path>     Path to jj repo (default: current directory)
  -h, --help        Print this help message
```

Uses jj-lib to resolve the changeID via revset, then prints the commit's full description text.

### Shared code

After refactoring, `src/main.rs` keeps:
- `USAGE` constant (updated to include `desc`)
- `Command` enum (with new `Desc` variant)
- `parse_args()` top-level dispatch
- `main()`

The jj-lib workspace/repo loading pattern (used by both `list` and `desc`) could become a small shared helper in `main.rs` or a `src/common.rs` if needed — we can decide in dev3.

---

     1→use std::io::Write;
     2→use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
     3→use std::process::{ExitCode, Stdio};
     4→
     5→use jj_lib::config::StackedConfig;
     6→use jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex;
     7→use jj_lib::object_id::ObjectId;
     8→use jj_lib::repo::{Repo, StoreFactories};
     9→use jj_lib::revset::{RevsetExpression, SymbolResolver};
    10→use jj_lib::settings::UserSettings;
    11→use jj_lib::workspace::{Workspace, default_working_copy_factories};
    12→use pollster::FutureExt;
    13→
    14→const USAGE: &str = "\
    15→Usage: vc-x1 [OPTIONS] <COMMAND>
    16→
    17→Commands:
    18→  list [<path>]       List commits in a jj repo (defaults to current directory)
    19→  finalize [OPTIONS]  Squash working copy into target commit (daemonizes by default)
    20→
    21→Options:
    22→  -V, --version  Print version
    23→  -h, --help     Print this help message";
    24→
    25→const FINALIZE_USAGE: &str = "\
    26→Usage: vc-x1 finalize [OPTIONS]
    27→
    28→Squash source revision into target revision, optionally push afterward.
    29→By default, daemonizes and returns immediately.
    30→
    31→Options:
    32→  --repo <path>      Path to jj repo (default: current directory)
    33→  --source <revset>  Source revision to squash (default: @)
    34→  --target <revset>  Target revision to squash into (default: @-)
    35→  --delay <seconds>  Seconds to wait before squashing (default: 1)
    36→  --push             Push after squashing
    37→  --log <path>       Log file path (default: /tmp/vc-x1-finalize-<timestamp-millis>.log)
    38→  --foreground       Run in foreground instead of daemonizing
    39→  -h, --help         Print this help message";
    40→
    41→#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
    42→enum Command {
    43→    Version,
    44→    Help,
    45→    FinalizeHelp,
    46→    List { path: PathBuf },
    47→    Finalize(FinalizeOpts),
    48→}
    49→
    50→#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
    51→struct FinalizeOpts {
    52→    repo: PathBuf,
    53→    source: String,
    54→    target: String,
    55→    delay_secs: f64,
    56→    push: bool,
    57→    log: PathBuf,
    58→    foreground: bool,
    59→    exec: bool,
    60→}
    61→
    62→impl Default for FinalizeOpts {
    63→    fn default() -> Self {
    64→        Self {
    65→            repo: PathBuf::from("."),
    66→            source: "@".to_string(),
    67→            target: "@-".to_string(),
    68→            delay_secs: 1.0,
    69→            push: false,
    70→            log: PathBuf::from(format!(
    71→                "/tmp/vc-x1-finalize-{}.log",
    72→                std::time::SystemTime::now()
    73→                    .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
    74→                    .unwrap_or_default()
    75→                    .as_millis()
    76→            )),
    77→            foreground: false,
    78→            exec: false,
    79→        }
    80→    }
    81→}
    82→
    83→fn parse_finalize_args<I>(args: &mut I) -> Result<Command, String>
    84→where
    85→    I: Iterator<Item = String>,
    86→{
    87→    let mut opts = FinalizeOpts::default();
    88→
    89→    while let Some(arg) = args.next() {
    90→        match arg.as_str() {
    91→            "--help" | "-h" => return Ok(Command::FinalizeHelp),
    92→            "--repo" => {
    93→                opts.repo = PathBuf::from(
    94→                    args.next()
    95→                        .ok_or_else(|| "--repo requires a value".to_string())?,
    96→                );
    97→            }
    98→            "--source" => {
    99→                opts.source = args
   100→                    .next()
   101→                    .ok_or_else(|| "--source requires a value".to_string())?;
   102→            }
   103→            "--target" => {
   104→                opts.target = args
   105→                    .next()
   106→                    .ok_or_else(|| "--target requires a value".to_string())?;
   107→            }
   108→            "--delay" => {
   109→                let val = args
   110→                    .next()
   111→                    .ok_or_else(|| "--delay requires a value".to_string())?;
   112→                opts.delay_secs = val
   113→                    .parse()
   114→                    .map_err(|_| format!("invalid delay value: {val}"))?;
   115→            }
   116→            "--push" => opts.push = true,
   117→            "--log" => {
   118→                opts.log = PathBuf::from(
   119→                    args.next()
   120→                        .ok_or_else(|| "--log requires a value".to_string())?,
   121→                );
   122→            }
   123→            "--foreground" => opts.foreground = true,
   124→            "--exec" => opts.exec = true,
   125→            other => {
   126→                return Err(format!(
   127→                    "unknown finalize option: {other}\n\n{FINALIZE_USAGE}"
   128→                ));
   129→            }
   130→        }
   131→    }
   132→
   133→    Ok(Command::Finalize(opts))
   134→}
   135→
   136→fn parse_args<I>(args: I) -> Result<Command, String>
   137→where
   138→    I: IntoIterator<Item = String>,
   139→{
   140→    let mut args = args.into_iter();
   141→    let _program = args.next();
   142→
   143→    match args.next().as_deref() {
   144→        Some("--version" | "-V") => Ok(Command::Version),
   145→        Some("--help" | "-h" | "help") => Ok(Command::Help),
   146→        Some("list") => {
   147→            let path = match args.next() {
   148→                Some(p) => PathBuf::from(p),
   149→                None => std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
   150→            };
   151→            Ok(Command::List { path })
   152→        }
   153→        Some("finalize") => parse_finalize_args(&mut args),
   154→        Some(other) => Err(format!("unknown command: {other}\n\n{USAGE}")),
   155→        None => Ok(Command::Help),
   156→    }
   157→}
   158→
   159→fn list(path: &Path) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   160→    let config = StackedConfig::with_defaults();
   161→    let settings = UserSettings::from_config(config)?;
   162→    let store_factories = StoreFactories::default();
   163→    let working_copy_factories = default_working_copy_factories();
   164→
   165→    let workspace = Workspace::load(&settings, path, &store_factories, &working_copy_factories)?;
   166→    let repo = workspace.repo_loader().load_at_head().block_on()?;
   167→
   168→    let expression = RevsetExpression::all();
   169→    let no_extensions: &[Box<dyn jj_lib::revset::SymbolResolverExtension>] = &[];
   170→    let symbol_resolver = SymbolResolver::new(repo.as_ref(), no_extensions);
   171→    let resolved = expression.resolve_user_expression(repo.as_ref(), &symbol_resolver)?;
   172→    let revset = resolved.evaluate(repo.as_ref())?;
   173→
   174→    let root_commit_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();
   175→
   176→    for result in revset.iter() {
   177→        let commit_id = result?;
   178→        if commit_id == root_commit_id {
   179→            continue;
   180→        }
   181→        let commit = repo.store().get_commit(&commit_id)?;
   182→
   183→        let change_hex = encode_reverse_hex(commit.change_id().as_bytes());
   184→        let change_short = &change_hex[..change_hex.len().min(12)];
   185→
   186→        let commit_hex = commit.id().hex();
   187→        let commit_short = &commit_hex[..commit_hex.len().min(12)];
   188→
   189→        let first_line = commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or("");
   190→
   191→        println!("{} {} {}", change_short, commit_short, first_line);
   192→    }
   193→
   194→    Ok(())
   195→}
   196→
   197→fn log_msg(log: &Path, msg: &str) {
   198→    let nanos = std::time::SystemTime::now()
   199→        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
   200→        .unwrap_or_default()
   201→        .as_nanos();
   202→    let pid = std::process::id();
   203→    let line = format!("[{nanos}] pid={pid} {msg}\n");
   204→    if let Ok(mut f) = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
   205→        .create(true)
   206→        .append(true)
   207→        .open(log)
   208→    {
   209→        let _ = f.write_all(line.as_bytes());
   210→    }
   211→}
   212→
   213→fn run_jj(args: &[&str], log: &Path) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   214→    let args_str = args.join(" ");
   215→    log_msg(log, &format!("run_jj: jj {args_str}"));
   216→    let output = std::process::Command::new("jj")
   217→        .args(args)
   218→        .output()
   219→        .map_err(|e| format!("failed to run jj: {e}"))?;
   220→    let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
   221→    let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
   222→    if !stdout.is_empty() {
   223→        log_msg(log, &format!("run_jj: stdout: {stdout}"));
   224→    }
   225→    if !stderr.is_empty() {
   226→        log_msg(log, &format!("run_jj: stderr: {stderr}"));
   227→    }
   228→    if !output.status.success() {
   229→        return Err(format!("jj {args_str} failed (exit {}): {stderr}", output.status).into());
   230→    }
   231→    Ok(())
   232→}
   233→
   234→fn finalize_exec(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   235→    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("finalize_exec: starting opts={opts:?}"));
   236→
   237→    // Sleep to let trailing writes settle
   238→    let delay = std::time::Duration::from_secs_f64(opts.delay_secs);
   239→    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("finalize_exec: sleeping {delay:?}"));
   240→    std::thread::sleep(delay);
   241→
   242→    // Squash source into target
   243→    let repo_str = opts.repo.to_string_lossy();
   244→    run_jj(
   245→        &[
   246→            "squash",
   247→            "--ignore-immutable",
   248→            "--use-destination-message",
   249→            "--from",
   250→            &opts.source,
   251→            "--into",
   252→            &opts.target,
   253→            "-R",
   254→            &repo_str,
   255→        ],
   256→        &opts.log,
   257→    )?;
   258→
   259→    if opts.push {
   260→        // Advance main bookmark to target, then push
   261→        run_jj(
   262→            &[
   263→                "bookmark",
   264→                "set",
   265→                "main",
   266→                "-r",
   267→                &opts.target,
   268→                "-R",
   269→                &repo_str,
   270→            ],
   271→            &opts.log,
   272→        )?;
   273→        run_jj(&["git", "push", "-R", &repo_str], &opts.log)?;
   274→    }
   275→
   276→    log_msg(&opts.log, "finalize_exec: done");
   277→    Ok(())
   278→}
   279→
   280→fn build_exec_args(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Vec<String> {
   281→    let mut args = vec![
   282→        "finalize".to_string(),
   283→        "--exec".to_string(),
   284→        "--repo".to_string(),
   285→        opts.repo.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   286→        "--source".to_string(),
   287→        opts.source.clone(),
   288→        "--target".to_string(),
   289→        opts.target.clone(),
   290→        "--delay".to_string(),
   291→        opts.delay_secs.to_string(),
   292→        "--log".to_string(),
   293→        opts.log.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   294→    ];
   295→    if opts.push {
   296→        args.push("--push".to_string());
   297→    }
   298→    args
   299→}
   300→
   301→/// Spawn a detached child process with `--exec` and return immediately.
   302→/// The child re-enters `main()`, parses `--exec`, and `finalize()` routes
   303→/// it to `finalize_exec()` where the actual work happens.
   304→fn daemonize(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   305→    log_msg(
   306→        &opts.log,
   307→        &format!("daemonize: parent starting opts={opts:?}"),
   308→    );
   309→
   310→    let exe = std::env::current_exe()?;
   311→    let args = build_exec_args(opts);
   312→    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("daemonize: exe={exe:?} args={args:?}"));
   313→
   314→    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new(exe);
   315→    cmd.args(&args).stdin(Stdio::null());
   316→
   317→    #[cfg(unix)]
   318→    {
   319→        use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;
   320→        unsafe {
   321→            cmd.pre_exec(|| {
   322→                libc::setsid();
   323→                Ok(())
   324→            });
   325→        }
   326→    }
   327→
   328→    let child = cmd.spawn()?;
   329→    log_msg(
   330→        &opts.log,
   331→        &format!("daemonize: spawned child pid={}", child.id()),
   332→    );
   333→    eprintln!(
   334→        "finalize: daemonized (pid {}), log: {}",
   335→        child.id(),
   336→        opts.log.display()
   337→    );
   338→    Ok(())
   339→}
   340→
   341→fn finalize(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   342→    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("finalize: entry opts={opts:?}"));
   343→    let result = if opts.foreground || opts.exec {
   344→        finalize_exec(opts)
   345→    } else {
   346→        daemonize(opts)
   347→    };
   348→    match &result {
   349→        Ok(()) => log_msg(&opts.log, "finalize: exit ok"),
   350→        Err(e) => log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("finalize: exit err={e}")),
   351→    }
   352→    result
   353→}
   354→
   355→fn main() -> ExitCode {
   356→    let cmd = match parse_args(std::env::args()) {
   357→        Ok(cmd) => cmd,
   358→        Err(e) => {
   359→            eprintln!("error: {e}");
   360→            return ExitCode::FAILURE;
   361→        }
   362→    };
   363→
   364→    match cmd {
   365→        Command::Version => {
   366→            println!("vc-x1 {}", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"));
   367→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
   368→        }
   369→        Command::Help => {
   370→            println!("{USAGE}");
   371→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
   372→        }
   373→        Command::FinalizeHelp => {
   374→            println!("{FINALIZE_USAGE}");
   375→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
   376→        }
   377→        Command::List { path } => {
   378→            if let Err(e) = list(&path) {
   379→                eprintln!("error: {e}");
   380→                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
   381→            }
   382→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
   383→        }
   384→        Command::Finalize(opts) => {
   385→            log_msg(&opts.log, "main: finalize entry");
   386→            match finalize(&opts) {
   387→                Ok(()) => {
   388→                    log_msg(&opts.log, "main: finalize exit ok");
   389→                    ExitCode::SUCCESS
   390→                }
   391→                Err(e) => {
   392→                    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("main: finalize exit err={e}"));
   393→                    eprintln!("error: {e}");
   394→                    ExitCode::FAILURE
   395→                }
   396→            }
   397→        }
   398→    }
   399→}

  "content": "use std::path::Path;\nuse std::sync::Arc;\n\nuse jj_lib::config::StackedConfig;\nuse jj_lib::repo::{ReadonlyRepo, StoreFactories};\nuse jj_lib::settings::UserSettings;\nuse jj_lib::workspace::{Workspace, default_working_copy_factories};\nuse pollster::FutureExt;\n\npub fn load_repo(path: &Path) -> Result<(Workspace, Arc<ReadonlyRepo>), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let config = StackedConfig::with_defaults();\n    let settings = UserSettings::from_config(config)?;\n    let store_factories = StoreFactories::default();\n    let working_copy_factories = default_working_copy_factories();\n\n    let workspace = Workspace::load(&settings, path, &store_factories, &working_copy_factories)?;\n    let repo = workspace.repo_loader().load_at_head().block_on()?;\n\n    Ok((workspace, repo))\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/src/list.rs",
  "content": "use std::path::Path;\n\nuse jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex;\nuse jj_lib::object_id::ObjectId;\nuse jj_lib::repo::Repo;\nuse jj_lib::revset::{RevsetExpression, SymbolResolver};\n\nuse crate::common;\n\npub fn list(path: &Path) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let (_workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(path)?;\n\n    let expression = RevsetExpression::all();\n    let no_extensions: &[Box<dyn jj_lib::revset::SymbolResolverExtension>] = &[];\n    let symbol_resolver = SymbolResolver::new(repo.as_ref(), no_extensions);\n    let resolved = expression.resolve_user_expression(repo.as_ref(), &symbol_resolver)?;\n    let revset = resolved.evaluate(repo.as_ref())?;\n\n    let root_commit_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();\n\n    for result in revset.iter() {\n        let commit_id = result?;\n        if commit_id == root_commit_id {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let commit = repo.store().get_commit(&commit_id)?;\n\n        let change_hex = encode_reverse_hex(commit.change_id().as_bytes());\n        let change_short = &change_hex[..change_hex.len().min(12)];\n\n        let commit_hex = commit.id().hex();\n        let commit_short = &commit_hex[..commit_hex.len().min(12)];\n\n        let first_line = commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n\n        println!(\"{} {} {}\", change_short, commit_short, first_line);\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n"
}

<COMMAND>\n\nCommands:\n  list [<path>]       List commits in a jj repo (defaults to current directory)\n  finalize [OPTIONS]  Squash working copy into target commit (daemonizes by default)\n\nOptions:\n  -V, --version  Print version\n  -h, --help     Print this help message\";\n\nconst FINALIZE_USAGE: &str = \"\\\nUsage: vc-x1 finalize [OPTIONS]\n\nSquash source revision into target revision, optionally push afterward.\nBy default, daemonizes and returns immediately.\n\nOptions:\n  --repo <path>      Path to jj repo (default: current directory)\n  --source <revset>  Source revision to squash (default: @)\n  --target <revset>  Target revision to squash into (default: @-)\n  --delay <seconds>  Seconds to wait before squashing (default: 1)\n  --push             Push after squashing\n  --log <path>       Log file path (default: /tmp/vc-x1-finalize-<timestamp-millis>.log)\n  --foreground       Run in foreground instead of daemonizing\n  -h, --help         Print this help message\";\n\n#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]\nenum Command {\n    Version,\n    Help,\n    FinalizeHelp,\n    List { path: PathBuf },\n    Finalize(FinalizeOpts),\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]\nstruct FinalizeOpts {\n    repo: PathBuf,\n    source: String,\n    target: String,\n    delay_secs: f64,\n    push: bool,\n    log: PathBuf,\n    foreground: bool,\n    exec: foreground: false,\n            exec: false,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfn parse_finalize_args<I>(args: &mut I) -> Result<Command, String>\nwhere\n    I: Iterator<Item = String>,\n{\n    let mut opts = FinalizeOpts::default();\n\n    while let Some(arg) = args.next() {\n        match arg.as_str() {\n            \"--help\" | \"-h\" => return Ok(Command::FinalizeHelp),\n            \"--repo\" => {\n                opts.repo = PathBuf::from(\n                    args.next()\n                        .ok_or_else(|| \"--repo requires a value\".to_string())?,\n                );\n            }\n            \"--source\" => {\n                opts.source = args\n                    .next()\n                    .ok_or_else(|| \"--source requires a value\".to_string())?;\n            }\n            \"--target\" => {\n                opts.target = args\n                    .next()\n                    .ok_or_else(|| \"--target requires a value\".to_string())?;\n            }\n            \"--delay\" => {\n                let val = args\n                    .next()\n                    .ok_or_else(|| \"--delay requires a value\".to_string())?;\n                opts.delay_secs = val\n                    .parse()\n                    .map_err(|_| format!(\"invalid delay value: {val}\"))?;\n            }\n            \"--push\" => opts.push = true,\n            \"--log\" => {\n                opts.log = PathBuf::from(\n                    args.next()\n                        .ok_or_else(|| \"--log requires a value\".to_string())?,\n                );\n            }\n            \"--foreground\" => opts.foreground = true,\n            \"--exec\" => opts.exec = true,\n            other => {\n                return Err(format!(\n                    \"unknown finalize option: {other}\\n\\n{FINALIZE_USAGE}\"\n                ));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(Command::Finalize(opts))\n}\n\nfn parse_args<I>(args: I) -> Result<Command, String>\nwhere\n    I: IntoIterator<Item = String>,\n{\n    let mut args = args.into_iter();\n    let _program = args.next();\n\n    match args.next().as_deref() {\n        Some(\"--version\" | \"-V\") => Ok(Command::Version),\n        Some(\"--help\" | \"-h\" | \"help\") => Ok(Command::Help),\n        Some(\"list\") => {\n            let path = match args.next() {\n                Some(p) => PathBuf::from(p),\n                None => std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,\n            };\n            Ok(Command::List { path })\n        }\n        Some(\"finalize\") => parse_finalize_args(&mut args),\n        Some(other) => Err(format!(\"unknown command: {other}\\n\\n{USAGE}\")),\n        None => Ok(Command::Help),\n    }\n}\n\nfn log_msg(log: &Path, msg: &str) {\n    let nanos = std::time::SystemTime::now()\n        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)\n        .unwrap_or_default()\n        .as_nanos();\n    let pid = std::process::id();\n    let line = format!(\"[{nanos}] pid={pid} {msg}\\n\");\n    if let Ok(mut f) = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()\n        .create(true)\n        .append(true)\n        .open(log)\n    {\n        let _ = f.write_all(line.as_bytes());\n    }\n}\n\nfn run_jj(args: &[&str], log: &Path) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let args_str = args.join(\" \");\n    log_msg(log, &format!(\"run_jj: jj {args_str}\"));\n    let output = std::process::Command::new(\"jj\")\n        .args(args)\n        .output()\n        .map_err(|e| format!(\"failed to run jj: {e}\"))?;\n    let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);\n    let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);\n    if !stdout.is_empty() {\n        log_msg(log, &format!(\"run_jj: stdout: {stdout}\"));\n    }\n    if !stderr.is_empty() {\n        log_msg(log, &format!(\"run_jj: stderr: {stderr}\"));\n    }\n    if !output.status.success() {\n        return Err(format!(\"jj {args_str} failed (exit {}): {stderr}\", output.status).into());\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn finalize_exec(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!(\"finalize_exec: starting opts={opts:?}\"));\n\n    // Sleep to let trailing writes settle\n    let delay = std::time::Duration::from_secs_f64(opts.delay_secs);\n    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!(\"finalize_exec: sleeping {delay:?}\"));\n    std::thread::sleep(delay);\n\n    // Squash source into target\n    let repo_str = opts.repo.to_string_lossy();\n    run_jj(\n        &[\n            \"squash\",\n         &repo_str,\n        ],\n        &opts.log,\n    )?;\n\n    if opts.push {\n        // Advance main bookmark to target, then push\n        run_jj(\n            &[\n                \"bookmark\",\n                \"set\",\n                \"main\",\n                \"-r\",\n                &opts.target,\n                \"-R\",\n                &repo_str,\n            ],\n            &opts.log,\n        )?;\n        run_jj(&[\"git\", \"push\", \"-R\", &repo_str], &opts.log)?;\n    }\n\n    log_msg(&opts.log, \"finalize_exec: done\");\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn build_exec_args(opts: opts.repo.to_string_lossy().to_string(),\n        \"--source\".to_string(),\n        opts.source.clone(),\n        \"--target\".to_string(),\n        opts.target.clone(),\n        \"--delay\".to_string(),\n        opts.delay_secs.to_string(),\n        \"--log\".to_string(),\n        opts.log.to_string_lossy().to_string(),\n    ];\n    if opts.push {\n          assert!(parsed.exec);\n        } else {\n            panic!(\"expected Finalize\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn parse_finalize_unknown_opt() {\n        let result = parse_args(args(&[\"vc-x1\", \"finalize\", \"--bogus\"]));\n        assert!(result.is_err());\n        assert!(result.unwrap_err().contains(\"unknown finalize option\"));\n    }\n}\n"
}

     1→mod common;
     2→mod list;
     3→
     4→use std::io::Write;
     5→use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
     6→use std::process::{ExitCode, Stdio};
     7→
     8→const USAGE: &str = "\
     9→Usage: vc-x1 [OPTIONS] <COMMAND>
    10→
    11→Commands:
    12→  list [<path>]       List commits in a jj repo (defaults to current directory)
    13→  finalize [OPTIONS]  Squash working copy into target commit (daemonizes by default)
    14→
    15→Options:
    16→  -V, --version  Print version
    17→  -h, --help     Print this help message";
    18→
    19→const FINALIZE_USAGE: &str = "\
    20→Usage: vc-x1 finalize [OPTIONS]
    21→
    22→Squash source revision into target revision, optionally push afterward.
    23→By default, daemonizes and returns immediately.
    24→
    25→Options:
    26→  --repo <path>      Path to jj repo (default: current directory)
    27→  --source <revset>  Source revision to squash (default: @)
    28→  --target <revset>  Target revision to squash into (default: @-)
    29→  --delay <seconds>  Seconds to wait before squashing (default: 1)
    30→  --push             Push after squashing
    31→  --log <path>       Log file path (default: /tmp/vc-x1-finalize-<timestamp-millis>.log)
    32→  --foreground       Run in foreground instead of daemonizing
    33→  -h, --help         Print this help message";
    34→
    35→#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
    36→enum Command {
    37→    Version,
    38→    Help,
    39→    FinalizeHelp,
    40→    List { path: PathBuf },
    41→    Finalize(FinalizeOpts),
    42→}
    43→
    44→#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
    45→struct FinalizeOpts {
    46→    repo: PathBuf,
    47→    source: String,
    48→    target: String,
    49→    delay_secs: f64,
    50→    push: bool,
    51→    log: PathBuf,
    52→    foreground: bool,
    53→    exec: bool,
    54→}
    55→
    56→impl Default for FinalizeOpts {
    57→    fn default() -> Self {
    58→        Self {
    59→            repo: PathBuf::from("."),
    60→            source: "@".to_string(),
    61→            target: "@-".to_string(),
    62→            delay_secs: 1.0,
    63→            push: false,
    64→            log: PathBuf::from(format!(
    65→                "/tmp/vc-x1-finalize-{}.log",
    66→                std::time::SystemTime::now()
    67→                    .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
    68→                    .unwrap_or_default()
    69→                    .as_millis()
    70→            )),
    71→            foreground: false,
    72→            exec: false,
    73→        }
    74→    }
    75→}
    76→
    77→fn parse_finalize_args<I>(args: &mut I) -> Result<Command, String>
    78→where
    79→    I: Iterator<Item = String>,
    80→{
    81→    let mut opts = FinalizeOpts::default();
    82→
    83→    while let Some(arg) = args.next() {
    84→        match arg.as_str() {
    85→            "--help" | "-h" => return Ok(Command::FinalizeHelp),
    86→            "--repo" => {
    87→                opts.repo = PathBuf::from(
    88→                    args.next()
    89→                        .ok_or_else(|| "--repo requires a value".to_string())?,
    90→                );
    91→            }
    92→            "--source" => {
    93→                opts.source = args
    94→                    .next()
    95→                    .ok_or_else(|| "--source requires a value".to_string())?;
    96→            }
    97→            "--target" => {
    98→                opts.target = args
    99→                    .next()
   100→                    .ok_or_else(|| "--target requires a value".to_string())?;
   101→            }
   102→            "--delay" => {
   103→                let val = args
   104→                    .next()
   105→                    .ok_or_else(|| "--delay requires a value".to_string())?;
   106→                opts.delay_secs = val
   107→                    .parse()
   108→                    .map_err(|_| format!("invalid delay value: {val}"))?;
   109→            }
   110→            "--push" => opts.push = true,
   111→            "--log" => {
   112→                opts.log = PathBuf::from(
   113→                    args.next()
   114→                        .ok_or_else(|| "--log requires a value".to_string())?,
   115→                );
   116→            }
   117→            "--foreground" => opts.foreground = true,
   118→            "--exec" => opts.exec = true,
   119→            other => {
   120→                return Err(format!(
   121→                    "unknown finalize option: {other}\n\n{FINALIZE_USAGE}"
   122→                ));
   123→            }
   124→        }
   125→    }
   126→
   127→    Ok(Command::Finalize(opts))
   128→}
   129→
   130→fn parse_args<I>(args: I) -> Result<Command, String>
   131→where
   132→    I: IntoIterator<Item = String>,
   133→{
   134→    let mut args = args.into_iter();
   135→    let _program = args.next();
   136→
   137→    match args.next().as_deref() {
   138→        Some("--version" | "-V") => Ok(Command::Version),
   139→        Some("--help" | "-h" | "help") => Ok(Command::Help),
   140→        Some("list") => {
   141→            let path = match args.next() {
   142→                Some(p) => PathBuf::from(p),
   143→                None => std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
   144→            };
   145→            Ok(Command::List { path })
   146→        }
   147→        Some("finalize") => parse_finalize_args(&mut args),
   148→        Some(other) => Err(format!("unknown command: {other}\n\n{USAGE}")),
   149→        None => Ok(Command::Help),
   150→    }
   151→}
   152→
   153→fn log_msg(log: &Path, msg: &str) {
   154→    let nanos = std::time::SystemTime::now()
   155→        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
   156→        .unwrap_or_default()
   157→        .as_nanos();
   158→    let pid = std::process::id();
   159→    let line = format!("[{nanos}] pid={pid} {msg}\n");
   160→    if let Ok(mut f) = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
   161→        .create(true)
   162→        .append(true)
   163→        .open(log)
   164→    {
   165→        let _ = f.write_all(line.as_bytes());
   166→    }
   167→}
   168→
   169→fn run_jj(args: &[&str], log: &Path) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   170→    let args_str = args.join(" ");
   171→    log_msg(log, &format!("run_jj: jj {args_str}"));
   172→    let output = std::process::Command::new("jj")
   173→        .args(args)
   174→        .output()
   175→        .map_err(|e| format!("failed to run jj: {e}"))?;
   176→    let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
   177→    let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
   178→    if !stdout.is_empty() {
   179→        log_msg(log, &format!("run_jj: stdout: {stdout}"));
   180→    }
   181→    if !stderr.is_empty() {
   182→        log_msg(log, &format!("run_jj: stderr: {stderr}"));
   183→    }
   184→    if !output.status.success() {
   185→        return Err(format!("jj {args_str} failed (exit {}): {stderr}", output.status).into());
   186→    }
   187→    Ok(())
   188→}
   189→
   190→fn finalize_exec(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   191→    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("finalize_exec: starting opts={opts:?}"));
   192→
   193→    // Sleep to let trailing writes settle
   194→    let delay = std::time::Duration::from_secs_f64(opts.delay_secs);
   195→    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("finalize_exec: sleeping {delay:?}"));
   196→    std::thread::sleep(delay);
   197→
   198→    // Squash source into target
   199→    let repo_str = opts.repo.to_string_lossy();
   200→    run_jj(
   201→        &[
   202→            "squash",
   203→            "--ignore-immutable",
   204→            "--use-destination-message",
   205→            "--from",
   206→            &opts.source,
   207→            "--into",
   208→            &opts.target,
   209→            "-R",
   210→            &repo_str,
   211→        ],
   212→        &opts.log,
   213→    )?;
   214→
   215→    if opts.push {
   216→        // Advance main bookmark to target, then push
   217→        run_jj(
   218→            &[
   219→                "bookmark",
   220→                "set",
   221→                "main",
   222→                "-r",
   223→                &opts.target,
   224→                "-R",
   225→                &repo_str,
   226→            ],
   227→            &opts.log,
   228→        )?;
   229→        run_jj(&["git", "push", "-R", &repo_str], &opts.log)?;
   230→    }
   231→
   232→    log_msg(&opts.log, "finalize_exec: done");
   233→    Ok(())
   234→}
   235→
   236→fn build_exec_args(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Vec<String> {
   237→    let mut args = vec![
   238→        "finalize".to_string(),
   239→        "--exec".to_string(),
   240→        "--repo".to_string(),
   241→        opts.repo.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   242→        "--source".to_string(),
   243→        opts.source.clone(),
   244→        "--target".to_string(),
   245→        opts.target.clone(),
   246→        "--delay".to_string(),
   247→        opts.delay_secs.to_string(),
   248→        "--log".to_string(),
   249→        opts.log.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   250→    ];
   251→    if opts.push {
   252→        args.push("--push".to_string());
   253→    }
   254→    args
   255→}
   256→
   257→/// Spawn a detached child process with `--exec` and return immediately.
   258→/// The child re-enters `main()`, parses `--exec`, and `finalize()` routes
   259→/// it to `finalize_exec()` where the actual work happens.
   260→fn daemonize(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   261→    log_msg(
   262→        &opts.log,
   263→        &format!("daemonize: parent starting opts={opts:?}"),
   264→    );
   265→
   266→    let exe = std::env::current_exe()?;
   267→    let args = build_exec_args(opts);
   268→    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("daemonize: exe={exe:?} args={args:?}"));
   269→
   270→    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new(exe);
   271→    cmd.args(&args).stdin(Stdio::null());
   272→
   273→    #[cfg(unix)]
   274→    {
   275→        use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;
   276→        unsafe {
   277→            cmd.pre_exec(|| {
   278→                libc::setsid();
   279→                Ok(())
   280→            });
   281→        }
   282→    }
   283→
   284→    let child = cmd.spawn()?;
   285→    log_msg(
   286→        &opts.log,
   287→        &format!("daemonize: spawned child pid={}", child.id()),
   288→    );
   289→    eprintln!(
   290→        "finalize: daemonized (pid {}), log: {}",
   291→        child.id(),
   292→        opts.log.display()
   293→    );
   294→    Ok(())
   295→}
   296→
   297→fn finalize(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   298→    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("finalize: entry opts={opts:?}"));
   299→    let result = if opts.foreground || opts.exec {
   300→        finalize_exec(opts)
   301→    } else {
   302→        daemonize(opts)
   303→    };
   304→    match &result {
   305→        Ok(()) => log_msg(&opts.log, "finalize: exit ok"),
   306→        Err(e) => log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("finalize: exit err={e}")),
   307→    }
   308→    result
   309→}
   310→
   311→fn main() -> ExitCode {
   312→    let cmd = match parse_args(std::env::args()) {
   313→        Ok(cmd) => cmd,
   314→        Err(e) => {
   315→            eprintln!("error: {e}");
   316→            return ExitCode::FAILURE;
   317→        }
   318→    };
   319→
   320→    match cmd {
   321→        Command::Version => {
   322→            println!("vc-x1 {}", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"));
   323→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
   324→        }
   325→        Command::Help => {
   326→            println!("{USAGE}");
   327→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
   328→        }
   329→        Command::FinalizeHelp => {
   330→            println!("{FINALIZE_USAGE}");
   331→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
   332→        }
   333→        Command::List { path } => {
   334→            if let Err(e) = list::list(&path) {
   335→                eprintln!("error: {e}");
   336→                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
   337→            }
   338→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
   339→        }
   340→        Command::Finalize(opts) => {
   341→            log_msg(&opts.log, "main: finalize entry");
   342→            match finalize(&opts) {
   343→                Ok(()) => {
   344→                    log_msg(&opts.log, "main: finalize exit ok");
   345→                    ExitCode::SUCCESS
   346→                }
   347→                Err(e) => {
   348→                    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("main: finalize exit err={e}"));
   349→                    eprintln!("error: {e}");
   350→                    ExitCode::FAILURE
   351→                }
   352→            }
   353→        }
   354→    }
   355→}
   356→
   357→#[cfg(test)]
   358→mod tests {
   359→    use super::*;
   360→
   361→    fn args(strs: &[&str]) -> Vec<String> {
   362→        strs.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect()
   363→    }
   364→
   365→    #[test]
   366→    fn parse_version_long() {
   367→        assert_eq!(
   368→            parse_args(args(&["vc-x1", "--version"])),
   369→            Ok(Command::Version)
   370→        );
   371→    }
   372→
   373→    #[test]
   374→    fn parse_version_short() {
   375→        assert_eq!(parse_args(args(&["vc-x1", "-V"])), Ok(Command::Version));
   376→    }
   377→

Returns `Ok(None)` when help is requested.\npub fn parse_args<I>(args: &mut I) -> Result<Option<FinalizeOpts>, String>\nwhere\n    I: Iterator<Item = String>,\n{\n    let mut opts = FinalizeOpts::default();\n\n    while let Some(arg) = args.next() {\n        match arg.as_str() {\n            \"--help\" | \"-h\" => return Ok(None),\n            \"--repo\" => {\n                opts.repo = PathBuf::from(\n                    args.next()\n                        .ok_or_else(|| \"--repo requires a value\".to_string())?,\n                );\n            }\n \"--target\" => {\n                opts.target = args\n                    .next()\n                    .ok_or_else(|| \"--target requires a value\".to_string())?;\n            }\n \"--foreground\" => opts.foreground = true,\n            \"--exec\" => opts.exec = true,\n            other => {\n                return Err(format!(\"unknown finalize option: {other}\\n\\n{USAGE}\"));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(Some(opts))\n}\n\npub fn log_msg(log: &Path, msg: &str) {\n    let nanos = std::time::SystemTime::now()\n        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)\n        .unwrap_or_default()\n        .as_nanos();\n    let pid = std::process::id();\n    let line = format!(\"[{nanos}] pid={pid} {msg}\\n\");\n    if let Ok(mut f) = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()\n        .create(true)\n        .append(true)\n        .open(log)\n    {\n        let _ = f.write_all(line.as_bytes());\n    }\n}\n\nfn run_jj(args: &[&str], log: &Path) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let args_str = args.join(\" \");\n    log_msg(log, &format!(\"run_jj: jj {args_str}\"));\n    let output = std::process::Command::new(\"jj\")\n        .args(args)\n        .output()\n        .map_err(|e| format!(\"failed to run jj: {e}\"))?;\n    let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);\n    let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);\n    if !stdout.is_empty() {\n        log_msg(log, &format!(\"run_jj: stdout: {stdout}\"));\n    }\n    if !stderr.is_empty() {\n        log_msg(log, &format!(\"run_jj: stderr: {stderr}\"));\n    }\n    if !output.status.success() {\n        return Err(format!(\"jj {args_str} failed (exit {}): {stderr}\", output.status).into());\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn finalize_exec(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!(\"finalize_exec: starting opts={opts:?}\"));\n\n    // Sleep to let trailing writes settle\n    let delay = std::time::Duration::from_secs_f64(opts.delay_secs);\n    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!(\"finalize_exec: sleeping {delay:?}\"));\n    std::thread::sleep(delay);\n\n    // Squash source into target\n    let repo_str = opts.repo.to_string_lossy();\n    run_jj(\n        &[\n            \"squash\",\n            \"--ignore-immutable\",\n            \"--use-destination-message\",\n            \"--from\",\n            &opts.source,\n            \"--into\",\n            &opts.target,\n            \"-R\",\n            &repo_str,\n        ],\n        &opts.log,\n    )?;\n\n    if opts.push {\n        // Advance main bookmark to target, then push\n        run_jj(\n            &[\n                \"bookmark\",\n                \"set\",\n                \"main\",\n                \"-r\",\n                &opts.target,\n                \"-R\",\n                &repo_str,\n            ],\n            &opts.log,\n        )?;\n        run_jj(&[\"git\", \"push\", \"-R\", &repo_str], &opts.log)?;\n    }\n\n    log_msg(&opts.log, \"finalize_exec: done\");\n    Ok(())\n}\n\npub fn build_exec_args(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Vec<String> {\n    let mut args = vec![\n        \"finalize\".to_string(),\n        \"--exec\".to_string(),\n        \"--repo\".to_string(),\n        opts.repo.to_string_lossy().to_string(),\n        \"--source\".to_string(),\n        opts.source.clone(),\n        \"--target\".to_string(),\n        opts.target.clone(),\n        \"--delay\".to_string(),\n        opts.delay_secs.to_string(),\n        \"--log\".to_string(),\n        opts.log.to_string_lossy().to_string(),\n    ];\n    if opts.push {\n        args.push(\"--push\".to_string());\n    }\n    args\n}\n\n/// Spawn a detached child process with `--exec` and return immediately.\n/// The child re-enters `main()`, parses `--exec`, and `finalize()` routes\n/// it to `finalize_exec()` where the actual work happens.\nfn daemonize(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    log_msg(\n        &opts.log,\n        &format!(\"daemonize: parent starting opts={opts:?}\"),\n    );\n\n    let exe = std::env::current_exe()?;\n    let args = build_exec_args(opts);\n    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!(\"daemonize: exe={exe:?} args={args:?}\"));\n\n    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new(exe);\n    cmd.args(&args).stdin(Stdio::null());\n\n    #[cfg(unix)]\n    {\n        use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;\n        unsafe {\n            cmd.pre_exec(|| {\n                libc::setsid();\n std::error::Error>> {\n    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!(\"finalize: entry opts={opts:?}\"));\n    let result = if opts.foreground || opts.exec {\n        finalize_exec(opts)\n    } else {\n        daemonize(opts)\n    };\n    match &result {\n        Ok(()) => log_msg(&opts.log, \"finalize: exit ok\"),\n        Err(e) => log_msg(&opts.log, &format!(\"finalize: exit err={e}\")),\n    }\n    result\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    fn args(strs: &[&str]) -> Vec<String> {\n        strs.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect()\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn parse_defaults() {\n        let result = parse_args(&mut std::iter::empty::<String>());\n        assert_eq!(result, Ok(Some(FinalizeOpts::default())));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn parse_all_opts() {\n        let a = args(&[\n            \"--repo\", \".claude\", \"--source\", \"@\", \"--target\", \"@-\", \"--delay\", \"2.5\", \"--push\",\n            \"--log\", \"/tmp/test.log\",   "content": "---\nname: Always commit .claude repo before finalize\ndescription: Must jj commit the .claude repo with matching title and ochid BEFORE running vc-x1 finalize\ntype: feedback\n---\n\nAlways commit the .claude repo BEFORE running `vc-x1 finalize`.      4→

    13→## Lessons Learned
    14→
    15→- **Avoid duplicate commits in .claude repo**: Committing the bot session repo multiple times in one session can create duplicate-titled commits with other session commits interleaved, making squashing non-trivial. Prefer a single commit to .claude at session end.
    16→- **ochid trailers need updating after squash**: When squashing changes the target commit in .claude, the ochid reference in the app repo must be updated to point at the correct changeID.
    17→- **Don't modify .claude repo programmatically mid-session**: Session data updates continuously, so bot-initiated changes to .claude can create circular modifications.
