Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding. Let me create the final summary:

## Summary

I've found the complete API for resolving a jj changeID string to a commit in the jj-lib source (version 0.39.0 in the cargo registry). Here's what you need to know:

### 1. **Direct API: `repo.resolve_change_id_prefix()`**

The **simplest path** to resolve a changeID string is to use the `ReadonlyRepo`'s `resolve_change_id_prefix()` method:

**File**: `/home/wink/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/jj-lib-0.39.0/src/repo.rs` (lines 371-376)

```rust
pub fn resolve_change_id_prefix(
    &self,
    prefix: &HexPrefix,
) -> IndexResult<PrefixResolution<ResolvedChangeTargets>>
```

**Steps**:
1. Parse your changeID string (like "wmuxkqwu") into a `HexPrefix` using **reverse-hex decoding**:
   ```rust
   let prefix = HexPrefix::try_from_reverse_hex("wmuxkqwu")?;
   ```

2. Call the repo's `resolve_change_id_prefix()`:
   ```rust
   let resolution = repo.resolve_change_id_prefix(&prefix)?;
   ```

3. Handle the result (enum `PrefixResolution<ResolvedChangeTargets>`):
   - `PrefixResolution::SingleMatch(ResolvedChangeTargets)` — unique match found
   - `PrefixResolution::AmbiguousMatch` — multiple matches
   - `PrefixResolution::NoMatch` — not found

4. Extract commit IDs from `ResolvedChangeTargets`:
   ```rust
   pub struct ResolvedChangeTargets {
       pub targets: Vec<(CommitId, ResolvedChangeState)>,
   }
   ```

**File locations**:
- `/home/wink/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/jj-lib-0.39.0/src/index.rs` (lines 216-223)

---

### 2. **Via Revset Expression API**

If you want to use the revset system to resolve changeIDs:

**Files**:
- `/home/wink/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/jj-lib-0.39.0/src/revset.rs`
- `/home/wink/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/jj-lib-0.39.0/src/revset_parser.rs`

**Flow**:

a) **Parse the revset string**:
   ```rust
   use jj_lib::revset_parser::parse_program;
   let expr_node = parse_program("change_id(wmuxkqwu)")?;
   ```

b) **Convert to UserRevsetExpression** (the revset parser returns an ExpressionNode which gets converted)

c) **Resolve symbols**:
   ```rust
   use jj_lib::revset::SymbolResolver;
   let symbol_resolver = SymbolResolver::new(repo, &[]);
   let resolved_expr = user_expr.resolve_user_expression(repo, &symbol_resolver)?;
   ```

d) **Evaluate**:
   ```rust
   let revset = resolved_expr.evaluate(repo)?;
   let commits: Vec<CommitId> = revset.iter().collect();
   ```

---

### 3. **Key Types and Conversions**

**ChangeID string → HexPrefix**:
- Use `HexPrefix::try_from_reverse_hex(change_id_string)` — changeIDs use reverse-hex encoding
- File: `/home/wink/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/jj-lib-0.39.0/src/object_id.rs` (lines 156-162)

**CommitRef type** (used internally):
- File: `/home/wink/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/jj-lib-0.39.0/src/revset.rs` (lines 142-154)
- `RevsetCommitRef::ChangeId(HexPrefix)` — represents a changeID reference

**Symbol resolution**:
- `SymbolResolver` handles tags, bookmarks, git refs, and change/commit IDs
- Contains `ChangePrefixResolver` that specifically resolves changeID prefixes
- File: `/home/wink/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/jj-lib-0.39.0/src/revset.rs` (lines 2760-2810)

---

### 4. **Recommended Approach**

For your use case, **use the direct API** (Method 1):

```rust
use jj_lib::object_id::HexPrefix;

// Given change_id_string like "wmuxkqwu"
let prefix = HexPrefix::try_from_reverse_hex(change_id_string)
    .ok_or("Invalid changeID format")?;

let resolution = repo.resolve_change_id_prefix(&prefix)?;

match resolution {
    PrefixResolution::SingleMatch(targets) => {
        // targets.targets is Vec<(CommitId, ResolvedChangeState)>
        let visible_commits = targets.into_visible();
        // Process visible_commits
    }
    PrefixResolution::AmbiguousMatch => {
        // Handle ambiguous changeID
    }
    PrefixResolution::NoMatch => {
        // Not found
    }
}
```

This is more direct and efficient than the full revset parsing pipeline.

{
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/src/desc.rs",
  "content": "use std::path::Path;\n\nuse jj_lib::index::PrefixResolution;\nuse jj_lib::object_id::HexPrefix;\nuse jj_lib::repo::Repo;\n\nuse crate::common;\n\npub const USAGE: &str = \"\\\nUsage: vc-x1 desc <chid> [OPTIONS]\n\nDisplay the full description of a commit identified by its changeID.\n\nArguments:\n  <chid>            The jj changeID (or prefix) to look up\n\nOptions:\n  --repo <path>     Path to jj repo (default: current directory)\n  -h, --help        Print this help message\";\n\npub fn desc(chid: &str, repo_path: &Path) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let (_workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(repo_path)?;\n\n    let prefix = HexPrefix::try_from_reverse_hex(chid)\n        .ok_or_else(|| format!(\"invalid changeID: {chid}\"))?;\n\n    let resolution = repo.resolve_change_id_prefix(&prefix)?;\n\n    match resolution {\n        PrefixResolution::SingleMatch(targets) => {\n            let commit_ids = targets.into_visible();\n            if commit_ids.is_empty() {\n                return Err(format!(\"changeID {chid} has no visible commits\").into());\n            }\n            for commit_id in &commit_ids {\n                let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;\n                let desc = commit.description();\n                if desc.is_empty() {\n                    println!(\"(no description set)\");\n                } else {\n                    print!(\"{desc}\");\n                }\n            }\n            Ok(())\n        }\n        PrefixResolution::AmbiguousMatch => {\n            Err(format!(\"changeID prefix {chid} is ambiguous\").into())\n        }\n        PrefixResolution::NoMatch => Err(format!(\"no commit found for changeID {chid}\").into()),\n    }\n}\n"
}
```

    26→}
    27→
    28→fn parse_args<I>(args: I) -> Result<Command, String>
    29→where
    30→    I: IntoIterator<Item = String>,
    31→{
    32→    let mut args = args.into_iter();
    33→    let _program = args.next();
    34→
    35→    match args.next().as_deref() {
    36→        Some("--version" | "-V") => Ok(Command::Version),
    37→        Some("--help" | "-h" | "help") => Ok(Command::Help),
    38→        Some("list") => {
    39→            let path = match args.next() {
    40→                Some(p) => PathBuf::from(p),
    41→                None => std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    42→            };
    43→            Ok(Command::List { path })
    44→        }
    45→        Some("finalize") => match finalize::parse_args(&mut args)? {
    46→            Some(opts) => Ok(Command::Finalize(opts)),
    47→            None => Ok(Command::FinalizeHelp),
    48→        },
    49→        Some(other) => Err(format!("unknown command: {other}\n\n{USAGE}")),
    50→        None => Ok(Command::Help),
    51→    }
    52→}
    53→
    54→fn main() -> ExitCode {
    55→    let cmd = match parse_args(std::env::args()) {
    56→        Ok(cmd) => cmd,
    57→        Err(e) => {
    58→            eprintln!("error: {e}");
    59→            return ExitCode::FAILURE;
    60→        }
    61→    };
    62→
    63→    match cmd {
    64→        Command::Version => {
    65→            println!("vc-x1 {}", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"));
    66→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
    67→        }
    68→        Command::Help => {
    69→            println!("{USAGE}");
    70→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
    71→        }
    72→        Command::FinalizeHelp => {
    73→            println!("{}", finalize::USAGE);
    74→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
    75→        }
    76→        Command::List { path } => {
    77→            if let Err(e) = list::list(&path) {
    78→                eprintln!("error: {e}");
    79→                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
    80→            }
    81→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
    82→        }
    83→        Command::Finalize(opts) => {
    84→            finalize::log_msg(&opts.log, "main: finalize entry");
    85→            match finalize::finalize(&opts) {
    86→                Ok(()) => {
    87→                    finalize::log_msg(&opts.log, "main: finalize exit ok");
    88→                    ExitCode::SUCCESS
    89→                }
    90→                Err(e) => {
    91→                    finalize::log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("main: finalize exit err={e}"));
    92→                    eprintln!("error: {e}");
    93→                    ExitCode::FAILURE
    94→                }
    95→            }
    96→        }
    97→    }
    98→}
    99→
   100→#[cfg(test)]
   101→mod tests {
   102→    use super::*;
   103→
   104→    fn args(strs: &[&str]) -> Vec<String> {
   105→        strs.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect()
   106→    }
   107→
   108→    #[test]
   109→    fn parse_version_long() {
   110→        assert_eq!(
   111→            parse_args(args(&["vc-x1", "--version"])),
   112→            Ok(Command::Version)
   113→        );
   114→    }
   115→
   116→    #[test]
   117→    fn parse_version_short() {
   118→        assert_eq!(parse_args(args(&["vc-x1", "-V"])), Ok(Command::Version));
   119→    }
   120→
   121→    #[test]
   122→    fn parse_help_flag() {
   123→        assert_eq!(parse_args(args(&["vc-x1", "--help"])), Ok(Command::Help));
   124→    }
   125→
   126→    #[test]
   127→    fn parse_help_short() {
   128→        assert_eq!(parse_args(args(&["vc-x1", "-h"])), Ok(Command::Help));
   129→    }
   130→
   131→    #[test]
   132→    fn parse_help_subcommand() {
   133→        assert_eq!(parse_args(args(&["vc-x1", "help"])), Ok(Command::Help));
   134→    }
   135→
   136→    #[test]
   137→    fn parse_no_args_shows_help() {
   138→        assert_eq!(parse_args(args(&["vc-x1"])), Ok(Command::Help));
   139→    }
   140→
   141→    #[test]
   142→    fn parse_list_with_path() {
   143→        assert_eq!(
   144→            parse_args(args(&["vc-x1", "list", "/some/path"])),
   145→            Ok(Command::List {
   146→                path: PathBuf::from("/some/path")
   147→            })
   148→        );
   149→    }
   150→
   151→    #[test]
   152→    fn parse_list_no_path_uses_cwd() {
   153→        let result = parse_args(args(&["vc-x1", "list"]));
   154→        assert!(result.is_ok());
   155→        if let Ok(Command::List { path }) = result {
   156→            assert!(path.is_absolute());
   157→        } else {
   158→            panic!("expected List");
   159→        }
   160→    }
   161→
   162→    #[test]
   163→    fn parse_unknown_command() {
   164→        let result = parse_args(args(&["vc-x1", "bogus"]));
   165→        assert!(result.is_err());
   166→        assert!(result.unwrap_err().contains("unknown command: bogus"));
   167→    }
   168→
   169→    #[test]
   170→    fn parse_finalize_defaults() {
   171→        assert_eq!(
   172→            parse_args(args(&["vc-x1", "finalize"])),
   173→            Ok(Command::Finalize(finalize::FinalizeOpts::default()))
   174→        );

```
     1→# CLAUDE.md - Bot Instructions for hw-jjg-bot
     2→
     3→## Project Structure
     4→
     5→This project uses **two separate jj-git repos**:
     6→
     7→1. **App repo** (`/` — project root): Contains the application source code.
     8→2. **Bot session repo** (`/.claude/`): Contains Claude Code session data.
     9→
    10→Both repos are managed with `jj` (Jujutsu), which coexists with git.
    11→
    12→## Repo Paths (relative from project root)
    13→
    14→- App repo: `.` (project root)
    15→- Bot session repo: `.claude`
    16→  (symlink from `~/.claude/projects/<path-to-project-root>/.claude`)
    17→
    18→## Committing
    19→
    20→Use `-R` (`--repository`) at the end to target the correct repo. Use
    21→relative paths to reduce noise. Putting `-R` last keeps the verb/action
    22→visible at the start of the command.
    23→
    24→### App repo
    25→```
    26→jj commit -m "title" -m "body" -R .
    27→```
    28→
    29→### Bot session repo
    30→```
    31→jj commit -m "title" -m "body" -R .claude
    32→```
    33→
    34→## jj Basics
    35→
    36→- `jj st -R .` / `jj st -R .claude` — show working copy status
    37→- `jj log -R .` / `jj log -R .claude` — show commit log
    38→- `jj commit -m "title" -m "body" -R <repo>` — finalize working copy into a commit
    39→- `jj describe -m "title" -m "body" -R <repo>` — set description without committing
    40→- In jj, the working copy (@) is always a mutable commit being edited.
    41→  `jj commit` finalizes it and creates a new empty working copy on top.
    42→- The `.claude` repo always has uncommitted changes during an active
    43→  session because session data updates continuously.
    44→
    45→## Pre-commit Requirements
    46→
    47→### User approval
    48→
    49→Never execute commit commands without the user's explicit approval.
    50→Present the full commands for review first; only run them after the
    51→user confirms.
    52→
    53→### Versioning
    54→
    55→Every change must start with a version bump. See
    56→[Versioning during development](notes/README.md#versioning-during-development)
    57→for details. Get user approval on single-step vs multi-step before starting.
    58→
    59→### Pre-commit checklist
    60→
    61→Before proposing a commit, run all of the following and fix any issues:
    62→
    63→1. `cargo fmt`
    64→2. `cargo clippy`
    65→3. `cargo test`
    66→4. `cargo install --path .` (if applicable)
    67→5. Retest after install
    68→
    69→## Session End Workflows
    70→
    71→When the user asks to "commit both repos" or says they're done, commit
    72→both repos. Use the **same title** for both commits so they're easy to
    73→correlate. The body can differ: the app repo body should summarize code
    74→changes; the bot session repo body should note what was done in the
    75→session.
    76→
    77→```
    78→jj commit -m "shared title" -m "app body" -R .
    79→jj commit -m "shared title" -m "session body" -R .claude
    80→```
    81→
    82→When the user also asks to push, advance the `main` bookmarks to the
    83→new commits first, then push.
    84→
    85→```
    86→jj bookmark set main -r @- -R .
    87→jj bookmark set main -r @- -R .claude
    88→jj git push -R .
    89→jj git push -R .claude
    90→```
    91→
    92→### Finalize the .claude repo
    93→
    94→The last action in a session is to finalize the `.claude` repo. This
    95→squashes the working copy into the session commit and pushes. The delay
    96→gives a safety margin against any pending writes. Always use a short
    97→relative path for `--repo`.
    98→
    99→```
   100→vc-x1 finalize --repo .claude --delay 5 --push
   101→```
   102→
   103→Always end with a status line so the user knows the background process
   104→is running:
   105→
   106→```
   107→Finalize is running (pid <PID>, log `/tmp/vc-x1-finalize-<TIMESTAMP>.log`)
   108→```
   109→
```

> DEVELOPER

I'm thinking we need a bettern sub-command architecture maybe we should move to clap, thoughts?

> AGENT

Tradeoffs to consider:

**Current hand-rolled approach:**
- You deliberately avoided clap early on ("Add --version and -V flags using std lib (clap not a dependency)")
- Each new subcommand means ~20-30 lines of match/parsing boilerplate
- Help strings are manually maintained
- It works, but the `parse_args` in main + per-module `parse_args` pattern is getting repetitive

**clap (derive):**
- Subcommand structure becomes declarative — an enum with `#[derive(Parser)]`
- Help/usage auto-generated
- Validation (required args, conflicts, value types) is built-in
- But it's a heavy dependency (compile time hit)
- You'd lose the `Option<FinalizeOpts>` pattern for help — clap handles that differently

**My take:** With 3 subcommands and more coming as you build the jj interface layer, clap derive would pay for itself. The parsing boilerplate is already ~40% of main.rs. But you're also using jj-lib which already pulls in a lot of deps, so the compile time concern is probably marginal.

One middle option: keep hand-rolled but extract a small arg-parsing helper pattern in common, reducing the per-subcommand boilerplate without adding a dependency.

What's your preference — go clap, stay hand-rolled with better structure, or something else?

> DEVELOPER

go clap

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/notes/chores-01.md",
  "old_string": "splitting the 0.6.0 commit, but this is deferred for now.",
  "new_string": "splitting the 0.6.0 commit, but this is deferred for now.\n\n## Migrate CLI parsing to clap (0.8.0)\n\n### Why\n\nWith 3 subcommands (list, finalize, desc) and more planned as the jj\ninterface layer grows, the hand-rolled arg parsing is becoming a\nmaintenance burden:\n\n- ~40% of main.rs is parsing boilerplate\n- Each new subcommand needs 20-30 lines of match/parsing code\n- Help strings are manually maintained and can drift\n- Validation (required args, types, conflicts) is reimplemented each time\n\n### Tradeoffs considered\n\n**Hand-rolled (current):**\n- Zero dependencies for parsing — was a deliberate early choice\n- Full control over help output and error messages\n- Gets increasingly verbose as subcommands multiply\n\n**clap with derive macros:**\n- Declarative subcommand structure via `#[derive(Parser)]` enum\n- Auto-generated help, version, and error messages\n- Built-in validation for types, required args, conflicts\n- Heavy dependency but jj-lib already pulls in a large dep tree,\n  so marginal compile-time impact is small\n\n### Decision\n\nAdopt clap derive. The parsing boilerplate cost now outweighs the\nzero-dependency benefit, and the subcommand count will only grow.\nSingle-step release as 0.8.0 since the external behavior is unchanged."
}
```

     1→mod common;
     2→mod desc;
     3→mod finalize;
     4→mod list;
     5→
     6→use std::path::PathBuf;
     7→use std::process::ExitCode;
     8→
     9→const USAGE: &str = "\
    10→Usage: vc-x1 [OPTIONS] <COMMAND>
    11→
    12→Commands:
    13→  desc <chid> [OPTIONS]  Show full description of a commit by changeID
    14→  list [<path>]          List commits in a jj repo (defaults to current directory)
    15→  finalize [OPTIONS]     Squash working copy into target commit (daemonizes by default)
    16→
    17→Options:
    18→  -V, --version  Print version
    19→  -h, --help     Print this help message";
    20→
    21→#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
    22→enum Command {
    23→    Version,
    24→    Help,
    25→    DescHelp,
    26→    FinalizeHelp,
    27→    Desc { chid: String, repo: PathBuf },
    28→    List { path: PathBuf },
    29→    Finalize(finalize::FinalizeOpts),
    30→}
    31→
    32→fn parse_args<I>(args: I) -> Result<Command, String>
    33→where
    34→    I: IntoIterator<Item = String>,
    35→{
    36→    let mut args = args.into_iter();
    37→    let _program = args.next();
    38→
    39→    match args.next().as_deref() {
    40→        Some("--version" | "-V") => Ok(Command::Version),
    41→        Some("--help" | "-h" | "help") => Ok(Command::Help),
    42→        Some("desc") => {
    43→            let mut chid: Option<String> = None;
    44→            let mut repo = std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    45→            while let Some(arg) = args.next() {
    46→                match arg.as_str() {
    47→                    "--help" | "-h" => return Ok(Command::DescHelp),
    48→                    "--repo" => {
    49→                        repo = PathBuf::from(
    50→                            args.next()
    51→                                .ok_or_else(|| "--repo requires a value".to_string())?,
    52→                        );
    53→                    }
    54→                    other if other.starts_with('-') => {
    55→                        return Err(format!("unknown desc option: {other}\n\n{}", desc::USAGE));
    56→                    }
    57→                    _ => {
    58→                        if chid.is_some() {
    59→                            return Err(format!("unexpected argument: {arg}\n\n{}", desc::USAGE));
    60→                        }
    61→                        chid = Some(arg);
    62→                    }
    63→                }
    64→            }
    65→            let chid = chid.ok_or_else(|| format!("missing <chid> argument\n\n{}", desc::USAGE))?;
    66→            Ok(Command::Desc { chid, repo })
    67→        }
    68→        Some("list") => {
    69→            let path = match args.next() {
    70→                Some(p) => PathBuf::from(p),
    71→                None => std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    72→            };
    73→            Ok(Command::List { path })
    74→        }
    75→        Some("finalize") => match finalize::parse_args(&mut args)? {
    76→            Some(opts) => Ok(Command::Finalize(opts)),
    77→            None => Ok(Command::FinalizeHelp),
    78→        },
    79→        Some(other) => Err(format!("unknown command: {other}\n\n{USAGE}")),
    80→        None => Ok(Command::Help),
    81→    }
    82→}
    83→
    84→fn main() -> ExitCode {
    85→    let cmd = match parse_args(std::env::args()) {
    86→        Ok(cmd) => cmd,
    87→        Err(e) => {
    88→            eprintln!("error: {e}");
    89→            return ExitCode::FAILURE;
    90→        }
    91→    };
    92→
    93→    match cmd {
    94→        Command::Version => {
    95→            println!("vc-x1 {}", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"));
    96→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
    97→        }
    98→        Command::Help => {
    99→            println!("{USAGE}");
   100→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
   101→        }
   102→        Command::DescHelp => {
   103→            println!("{}", desc::USAGE);
   104→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
   105→        }
   106→        Command::FinalizeHelp => {
   107→            println!("{}", finalize::USAGE);
   108→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
   109→        }
   110→        Command::Desc { chid, repo } => {
   111→            if let Err(e) = desc::desc(&chid, &repo) {
   112→                eprintln!("error: {e}");
   113→                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
   114→            }
   115→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
   116→        }
   117→        Command::List { path } => {
   118→            if let Err(e) = list::list(&path) {
   119→                eprintln!("error: {e}");
   120→                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
   121→            }
   122→            ExitCode::SUCCESS
   123→        }
   124→        Command::Finalize(opts) => {
   125→            finalize::log_msg(&opts.log, "main: finalize entry");
   126→            match finalize::finalize(&opts) {
   127→                Ok(()) => {
   128→                    finalize::log_msg(&opts.log, "main: finalize exit ok");
   129→                    ExitCode::SUCCESS
   130→                }
   131→                Err(e) => {
   132→                    finalize::log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("main: finalize exit err={e}"));
   133→                    eprintln!("error: {e}");
   134→                    ExitCode::FAILURE
   135→                }
   136→            }
   137→        }
   138→    }
   139→}
   140→
   141→#[cfg(test)]
   142→mod tests {
   143→    use super::*;
   144→
   145→    fn args(strs: &[&str]) -> Vec<String> {
   146→        strs.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect()
   147→    }

     1→use std::io::Write;
     2→use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
     3→use std::process::Stdio;
     4→
     5→pub const USAGE: &str = "\
     6→Usage: vc-x1 finalize [OPTIONS]
     7→
     8→Squash source revision into target revision, optionally push afterward.
     9→By default, daemonizes and returns immediately.
    10→
    11→Options:
    12→  --repo <path>      Path to jj repo (default: current directory)
    13→  --source <revset>  Source revision to squash (default: @)
    14→  --target <revset>  Target revision to squash into (default: @-)
    15→  --delay <seconds>  Seconds to wait before squashing (default: 1)
    16→  --push             Push after squashing
    17→  --log <path>       Log file path (default: /tmp/vc-x1-finalize-<timestamp-millis>.log)
    18→  --foreground       Run in foreground instead of daemonizing
    19→  -h, --help         Print this help message";
    20→
    21→#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
    22→pub struct FinalizeOpts {
    23→    pub repo: PathBuf,
    24→    pub source: String,
    25→    pub target: String,
    26→    pub delay_secs: f64,
    27→    pub push: bool,
    28→    pub log: PathBuf,
    29→    pub foreground: bool,
    30→    pub exec: bool,
    31→}
    32→
    33→impl Default for FinalizeOpts {
    34→    fn default() -> Self {
    35→        Self {
    36→            repo: PathBuf::from("."),
    37→            source: "@".to_string(),
    38→            target: "@-".to_string(),
    39→            delay_secs: 1.0,
    40→            push: false,
    41→            log: PathBuf::from(format!(
    42→                "/tmp/vc-x1-finalize-{}.log",
    43→                std::time::SystemTime::now()
    44→                    .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
    45→                    .unwrap_or_default()
    46→                    .as_millis()
    47→            )),
    48→            foreground: false,
    49→            exec: false,
    50→        }
    51→    }
    52→}
    53→
    54→/// Parse finalize subcommand args. Returns `Ok(None)` when help is requested.
    55→pub fn parse_args<I>(args: &mut I) -> Result<Option<FinalizeOpts>, String>
    56→where
    57→    I: Iterator<Item = String>,
    58→{
    59→    let mut opts = FinalizeOpts::default();
    60→
    61→    while let Some(arg) = args.next() {
    62→        match arg.as_str() {
    63→            "--help" | "-h" => return Ok(None),
    64→            "--repo" => {
    65→                opts.repo = PathBuf::from(
    66→                    args.next()
    67→                        .ok_or_else(|| "--repo requires a value".to_string())?,
    68→                );
    69→            }
    70→            "--source" => {
    71→                opts.source = args
    72→                    .next()
    73→                    .ok_or_else(|| "--source requires a value".to_string())?;
    74→            }
    75→            "--target" => {
    76→                opts.target = args
    77→                    .next()
    78→                    .ok_or_else(|| "--target requires a value".to_string())?;
    79→            }
    80→            "--delay" => {
    81→                let val = args
    82→                    .next()
    83→                    .ok_or_else(|| "--delay requires a value".to_string())?;
    84→                opts.delay_secs = val
    85→                    .parse()
    86→                    .map_err(|_| format!("invalid delay value: {val}"))?;
    87→            }
    88→            "--push" => opts.push = true,
    89→            "--log" => {
    90→                opts.log = PathBuf::from(
    91→                    args.next()
    92→                        .ok_or_else(|| "--log requires a value".to_string())?,
    93→                );
    94→            }
    95→            "--foreground" => opts.foreground = true,
    96→            "--exec" => opts.exec = true,
    97→            other => {
    98→                return Err(format!("unknown finalize option: {other}\n\n{USAGE}"));
    99→            }
   100→        }
   101→    }
   102→
   103→    Ok(Some(opts))
   104→}
   105→
   106→pub fn log_msg(log: &Path, msg: &str) {
   107→    let nanos = std::time::SystemTime::now()
   108→        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
   109→        .unwrap_or_default()
   110→        .as_nanos();
   111→    let pid = std::process::id();
   112→    let line = format!("[{nanos}] pid={pid} {msg}\n");
   113→    if let Ok(mut f) = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
   114→        .create(true)
   115→        .append(true)
   116→        .open(log)
   117→    {
   118→        let _ = f.write_all(line.as_bytes());
   119→    }
   120→}
   121→
   122→fn run_jj(args: &[&str], log: &Path) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   123→    let args_str = args.join(" ");
   124→    log_msg(log, &format!("run_jj: jj {args_str}"));
   125→    let output = std::process::Command::new("jj")
   126→        .args(args)
   127→        .output()
   128→        .map_err(|e| format!("failed to run jj: {e}"))?;
   129→    let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
   130→    let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
   131→    if !stdout.is_empty() {
   132→        log_msg(log, &format!("run_jj: stdout: {stdout}"));
   133→    }
   134→    if !stderr.is_empty() {
   135→        log_msg(log, &format!("run_jj: stderr: {stderr}"));
   136→    }
   137→    if !output.status.success() {
   138→        return Err(format!("jj {args_str} failed (exit {}): {stderr}", output.status).into());
   139→    }
   140→    Ok(())
   141→}
   142→
   143→fn finalize_exec(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   144→    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("finalize_exec: starting opts={opts:?}"));
   145→
   146→    // Sleep to let trailing writes settle
   147→    let delay = std::time::Duration::from_secs_f64(opts.delay_secs);
   148→    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("finalize_exec: sleeping {delay:?}"));
   149→    std::thread::sleep(delay);
   150→
   151→    // Squash source into target
   152→    let repo_str = opts.repo.to_string_lossy();
   153→    run_jj(
   154→        &[
   155→            "squash",
   156→            "--ignore-immutable",
   157→            "--use-destination-message",
   158→            "--from",
   159→            &opts.source,
   160→            "--into",
   161→            &opts.target,
   162→            "-R",
   163→            &repo_str,
   164→        ],
   165→        &opts.log,
   166→    )?;
   167→
   168→    if opts.push {
   169→        // Advance main bookmark to target, then push
   170→        run_jj(
   171→            &[
   172→                "bookmark",
   173→                "set",
   174→                "main",
   175→                "-r",
   176→                &opts.target,
   177→                "-R",
   178→                &repo_str,
   179→            ],
   180→            &opts.log,
   181→        )?;
   182→        run_jj(&["git", "push", "-R", &repo_str], &opts.log)?;
   183→    }
   184→
   185→    log_msg(&opts.log, "finalize_exec: done");
   186→    Ok(())
   187→}
   188→
   189→pub fn build_exec_args(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Vec<String> {
   190→    let mut args = vec![
   191→        "finalize".to_string(),
   192→        "--exec".to_string(),
   193→        "--repo".to_string(),
   194→        opts.repo.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   195→        "--source".to_string(),
   196→        opts.source.clone(),
   197→        "--target".to_string(),
   198→        opts.target.clone(),
   199→        "--delay".to_string(),
   200→        opts.delay_secs.to_string(),
   201→        "--log".to_string(),
   202→        opts.log.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   203→    ];
   204→    if opts.push {
   205→        args.push("--push".to_string());
   206→    }
   207→    args
   208→}
   209→
   210→/// Spawn a detached child process with `--exec` and return immediately.
   211→/// The child re-enters `main()`, parses `--exec`, and `finalize()` routes
   212→/// it to `finalize_exec()` where the actual work happens.
   213→fn daemonize(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   214→    log_msg(
   215→        &opts.log,
   216→        &format!("daemonize: parent starting opts={opts:?}"),
   217→    );
   218→
   219→    let exe = std::env::current_exe()?;
   220→    let args = build_exec_args(opts);
   221→    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("daemonize: exe={exe:?} args={args:?}"));
   222→
   223→    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new(exe);
   224→    cmd.args(&args).stdin(Stdio::null());
   225→
   226→    #[cfg(unix)]
   227→    {
   228→        use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;
   229→        unsafe {
   230→            cmd.pre_exec(|| {
   231→                libc::setsid();
   232→                Ok(())
   233→            });
   234→        }
   235→    }
   236→
   237→    let child = cmd.spawn()?;
   238→    log_msg(
   239→        &opts.log,
   240→        &format!("daemonize: spawned child pid={}", child.id()),
   241→    );
   242→    eprintln!(
   243→        "finalize: daemonized (pid {}), log: {}",
   244→        child.id(),
   245→        opts.log.display()
   246→    );
   247→    Ok(())
   248→}
   249→
   250→pub fn finalize(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   251→    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("finalize: entry opts={opts:?}"));
   252→    let result = if opts.foreground || opts.exec {
   253→        finalize_exec(opts)
   254→    } else {
   255→        daemonize(opts)
   256→    };
   257→    match &result {
   258→        Ok(()) => log_msg(&opts.log, "finalize: exit ok"),
   259→        Err(e) => log_msg(&opts.log, &format!("finalize: exit err={e}")),
   260→    }
   261→    result
   262→}
   263→

     1→use std::path::Path;
     2→
     3→use jj_lib::object_id::HexPrefix;
     4→use jj_lib::object_id::PrefixResolution;
     5→use jj_lib::repo::Repo;
     6→
     7→use crate::common;
     8→
     9→pub const USAGE: &str = "\
    10→Usage: vc-x1 desc <chid> [OPTIONS]
    11→
    12→Display the full description of a commit identified by its changeID.
    13→
    14→Arguments:
    15→  <chid>            The jj changeID (or prefix) to look up
    16→
    17→Options:
    18→  --repo <path>     Path to jj repo (default: current directory)
    19→  -h, --help        Print this help message";
    20→
    21→pub fn desc(chid: &str, repo_path: &Path) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    22→    let (_workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(repo_path)?;
    23→
    24→    let prefix =
    25→        HexPrefix::try_from_reverse_hex(chid).ok_or_else(|| format!("invalid changeID: {chid}"))?;
    26→
    27→    let resolution = repo.resolve_change_id_prefix(&prefix)?;
    28→
    29→    match resolution {
    30→        PrefixResolution::SingleMatch(targets) => {
    31→            let commit_ids = targets
    32→                .into_visible()
    33→                .ok_or_else(|| format!("changeID {chid} has no visible commits"))?;
    34→            for commit_id in &commit_ids {
    35→                let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;
    36→                let desc = commit.description();
    37→                if desc.is_empty() {
    38→                    println!("(no description set)");
    39→                } else {
    40→                    print!("{desc}");
    41→                }
    42→            }
    43→            Ok(())
    44→        }
    45→        PrefixResolution::AmbiguousMatch => {
    46→            Err(format!("changeID prefix {chid} is ambiguous").into())
    47→        }
    48→        PrefixResolution::NoMatch => Err(format!("no commit found for changeID {chid}").into()),
    49→    }
    50→}
    51→

     1→use std::path::Path;
     2→
     3→use jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex;
     4→use jj_lib::object_id::ObjectId;
     5→use jj_lib::repo::Repo;
     6→use jj_lib::revset::{RevsetExpression, SymbolResolver};
     7→
     8→use crate::common;
     9→
    10→pub fn list(path: &Path) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    11→    let (_workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(path)?;
    12→
    13→    let expression = RevsetExpression::all();
    14→    let no_extensions: &[Box<dyn jj_lib::revset::SymbolResolverExtension>] = &[];
    15→    let symbol_resolver = SymbolResolver::new(repo.as_ref(), no_extensions);
    16→    let resolved = expression.resolve_user_expression(repo.as_ref(), &symbol_resolver)?;
    17→    let revset = resolved.evaluate(repo.as_ref())?;
    18→
    19→    let root_commit_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();
    20→
    21→    for result in revset.iter() {
    22→        let commit_id = result?;
    23→        if commit_id == root_commit_id {
    24→            continue;
    25→        }
    26→        let commit = repo.store().get_commit(&commit_id)?;
    27→
    28→        let change_hex = encode_reverse_hex(commit.change_id().as_bytes());
    29→        let change_short = &change_hex[..change_hex.len().min(12)];
    30→
    31→        let commit_hex = commit.id().hex();
    32→        let commit_short = &commit_hex[..commit_hex.len().min(12)];
    33→
    34→        let first_line = commit.description().lines().next().unwrap_or("");
    35→
    36→        println!("{} {} {}", change_short, commit_short, first_line);
    37→    }
    38→
    39→    Ok(())
    40→}
    41→
```

#[arg(long, default_value = \".\")]\n    pub repo: PathBuf,\n\n    /// Source revision to squash\n    #[arg(long, default_value = \"@\")]\n    pub source: String,\n\n    /// Target revision to squash into\n    #[arg(long, default_value = \"@-\")]\n    pub target: String,\n\n    /// Seconds to wait before squashing\n    #[arg(long, default_value_t = 1.0)]\n    pub delay: f64,\n\n    /// Push after squashing\n    #[arg(long)]\n    pub push: bool,\n\n    /// Log file path (default: /tmp/vc-x1-finalize-<timestamp-millis>.log)\n    #[arg(long)]\n    pub log: Option<PathBuf>,\n\n    /// Run in foreground instead of daemonizing\n    #[arg(long)]\n    pub foreground: bool,\n\n    /// Internal: run exec path (used by daemonize)\n    #[arg(long, hide = true)]\n    pub exec: bool,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct FinalizeOpts {\n    pub repo: PathBuf,\n    pub source: String,\n    pub target: String,\n    pub delay_secs: f64,\n    pub push: bool,\n    pub log: PathBuf,\n    pub foreground: bool,\n    pub exec: bool,\n}\n\nimpl FinalizeArgs {\n    pub fn into_opts(self) -> FinalizeOpts {\n        let log = self.log.unwrap_or_else(|| {\n            PathBuf::from(format!(\n                \"/tmp/vc-x1-finalize-{}.log\",\n                std::time::SystemTime::now()\n                    .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)\n                    .unwrap_or_default()\n                    .as_millis()\n            ))\n        });\n        FinalizeOpts {\n            repo: self.repo,\n            source: self.source,\n            target: self.target,\n            delay_secs: self.delay,\n            push: self.push,\n            log,\n            foreground: self.foreground,\n            exec: self.exec,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\npub fn log_msg(log: &Path, msg: &str) {\n    let nanos = std::time::SystemTime::now()\n        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)\n        .unwrap_or_default()\n        .as_nanos();\n    let pid = std::process::id();\n    let line = format!(\"[{nanos}] pid={pid} {msg}\\n\");\n    if let Ok(mut f) = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()\n        .create(true)\n        .append(true)\n        .open(log)\n    {\n        let _ = f.write_all(line.as_bytes());\n    }\n}\n\nfn run_jj(args: &[&str], log: &Path) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let args_str = args.join(\" \");\n    log_msg(log, &format!(\"run_jj: jj {args_str}\"));\n    let output = std::process::Command::new(\"jj\")\n        .args(args)\n        .output()\n        .map_err(|e| format!(\"failed to run jj: {e}\"))?;\n    let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);\n    let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);\n    if !stdout.is_empty() {\n        log_msg(log, &format!(\"run_jj: stdout: {stdout}\"));\n    }\n    if !stderr.is_empty() {\n        log_msg(log, &format!(\"run_jj: stderr: {stderr}\"));\n    }\n    if !output.status.success() {\n        return Err(format!(\"jj {args_str} failed (exit {}): {stderr}\", output.status).into());\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn finalize_exec(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!(\"finalize_exec: starting opts={opts:?}\"));\n\n    // Sleep to let trailing writes settle\n    let delay = std::time::Duration::from_secs_f64(opts.delay_secs);\n    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!(\"finalize_exec: sleeping {delay:?}\"));\n    std::thread::sleep(delay);\n\n    // Squash source into target\n    let repo_str = opts.repo.to_string_lossy();\n    run_jj(\n        &[\n            \"squash\",\n            \"--ignore-immutable\",\n            \"--use-destination-message\",\n            \"--from\",\n            &opts.source,\n            \"--into\",\n            &opts.target,\n            \"-R\",\n            &repo_str,\n        ],\n        &opts.log,\n    )?;\n\n    if opts.push {\n        // Advance main bookmark to target, then push\n        run_jj(\n            &[\n                \"bookmark\",\n                \"set\",\n                \"main\",\n                \"-r\",\n                &opts.target,\n                \"-R\",\n                &repo_str,\n            ],\n            &opts.log,\n        )?;\n        run_jj(&[\"git\", \"push\", \"-R\", &repo_str], &opts.log)?;\n    }\n\n    log_msg(&opts.log, \"finalize_exec: done\");\n    Ok(())\n}\n\npub fn build_exec_args(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Vec<String> {\n    let mut args = vec![\n        \"finalize\".to_string(),\n        \"--exec\".to_string(),\n        \"--repo\".to_string(),\n        opts.repo.to_string_lossy().to_string(),\n        \"--source\".to_string(),\n        opts.source.clone(),\n        \"--target\".to_string(),\n        opts.target.clone(),\n        \"--delay\".to_string(),\n        opts.delay_secs.to_string(),\n        \"--log\".to_string(),\n        opts.log.to_string_lossy().to_string(),\n    ];\n    if opts.push {\n        args.push(\"--push\".to_string());\n    }\n    args\n}\n\n/// Spawn a detached child process with `--exec` and return immediately.\n/// The child re-enters `main()`, parses `--exec`, and `finalize()` routes\n/// it to `finalize_exec()` where the actual work happens.\nfn daemonize(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    log_msg(\n        &opts.log,\n        &format!(\"daemonize: parent starting opts={opts:?}\"),\n    );\n\n    let exe = std::env::current_exe()?;\n    let args = build_exec_args(opts);\n    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!(\"daemonize: exe={exe:?} args={args:?}\"));\n\n    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new(exe);\n    cmd.args(&args).stdin(Stdio::null());\n\n    #[cfg(unix)]\n    {\n        use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;\n        unsafe {\n            cmd.pre_exec(|| {\n                libc::setsid();\n                Ok(())\n            });\n        }\n    }\n\n    let child = cmd.spawn()?;\n    log_msg(\n        &opts.log,\n        &format!(\"daemonize: spawned child pid={}\", child.id()),\n    );\n    eprintln!(\n        \"finalize: daemonized (pid {}), log: {}\",\n        child.id(),\n        opts.log.display()\n    );\n    Ok(())\n}\n\npub fn finalize(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    log_msg(&opts.log, &format!(\"finalize: entry opts={opts:?}\"));\n    let result = if opts.foreground || opts.exec {\n        finalize_exec(opts)\n    } else {\n        daemonize(opts)\n    };\n    match &result {\n        Ok(()) => log_msg(&opts.log, \"finalize: exit ok\"),\n        Err(e) => log_msg(&opts.log, &format!(\"finalize: exit err={e}\")),\n    }\n    result\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::Cli;\n    use clap::Parser;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn build_exec_args_roundtrip() {\n        let opts = FinalizeOpts {\n            repo: PathBuf::from(\".claude\"),\n            source: \"@\".to_string(),\n            target: \"@-\".to_string(),\n            delay_secs: 2.0,\n            push: true,\n            log: PathBuf::from(\"/tmp/test.log\"),\n            foreground: false,\n            exec: false,\n        };\n        let exec_args = build_exec_args(&opts);\n        let mut full_args = vec![\"vc-x1\".to_string()];\n        full_args.extend(exec_args);\n        let cli = Cli::try_parse_from(full_args).unwrap();\n        if let crate::Commands::Finalize(args) = cli.command {\n            let parsed = args.into_opts();\n            assert_eq!(parsed.repo, opts.repo);\n            assert_eq!(parsed.source, opts.source);\n            assert_eq!(parsed.target, opts.target);\n            assert_eq!(parsed.delay_secs, opts.delay_secs);\n            assert_eq!(parsed.push, opts.push);\n            assert_eq!(parsed.log, opts.log);\n            assert!(parsed.exec);\n        } else {\n            panic!(\"expected Finalize\");\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "use std::path::Path;\n\nuse jj_lib::object_id::HexPrefix;\nuse jj_lib::object_id::PrefixResolution;\nuse jj_lib::repo::Repo;\n\nuse crate::common;\n\npub fn desc(chid: &str, repo_path: &Path) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let (_workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(repo_path)?;\n\n    let prefix =\n        HexPrefix::try_from_reverse_hex(chid).ok_or_else(|| format!(\"invalid changeID: {chid}\"))?;\n\n    let resolution = repo.resolve_change_id_prefix(&prefix)?;\n\n    match resolution {\n        PrefixResolution::SingleMatch(targets) => {\n            let commit_ids = targets\n                .into_visible()\n                .ok_or_else(|| format!(\"changeID {chid} has no visible commits\"))?;\n            for commit_id in &commit_ids {\n                let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;\n                let desc = commit.description();\n                if desc.is_empty() {\n                    println!(\"(no description set)\");\n                } else {\n                    print!(\"{desc}\");\n                }\n            }\n            Ok(())\n        }\n        PrefixResolution::AmbiguousMatch => {\n            Err(format!(\"changeID prefix {chid} is ambiguous\").into())\n        }\n        PrefixResolution::NoMatch => Err(format!(\"no commit found for changeID {chid}\").into()),\n    }\n}\n"
}
